# Pain and Gain (season 4) — the bot's rules, history and tooling

Owner: the Pain and Gain session. Code: `starter/src/jsMain/kotlin/season4/painandgain/`, bindings
`types/src/jsMain/kotlin/screeps/api/season4/` (`ScoreFlag.kt`, `PainAndGainConstants.kt`), arena folder
`arenas/season4-pain_and_gain/`, stub harness `tools/stub/painandgain/`. Only this bot's session edits this file (see
the parallel-sessions rules in `CLAUDE.md`).

## Measured rules and design

**`season4/painandgain/`** — Season 4 "Pain and Gain" (basic) bot (first version 04.09.2026). **Rules** (the arena description in the client lobby; the website docs do not describe this mode, only the effect table in the client bundle `screeps_arena.app/Contents/Resources/app/arena-docs/index.html` does): each player has a **pre-set army of 14 creeps — no spawning, construction, energy or replacements**; seven neutral `ScoreFlag`s, captured by **standing on the cell**; a captured flag scores per tick for its owner and applies a global debuff to the owner's whole army, same-type flags stack: Vulnerability ×1 (5/tick, incoming combat damage ×1.1), Heal reduction ×2 (4/tick each, healing ×0.75/×0.5), Attack reduction ×2 (3/tick, ATTACK ×0.8/×0.6), Ranged reduction ×2 (3/tick, RANGED_ATTACK ×0.8/×0.6) — 25/tick in total (`MAX_SCORE_PER_TICK`); 2000 ticks; **win = higher score, or destroy all enemy creeps**; the match ends early when an army is destroyed or the lead is mathematically unreachable — measured 07.09.2026 on the v132 series: the tick the lead exceeds 25 × the remaining ticks (every flag's rate together), wins at 1859 and 1931 exactly there, losses at 1718–1983 with both armies whole; the bot's `maxSwing` is this rule, and a match at 1700–2000 with both armies alive is a points end, not an annihilation. API: `ScoreFlag` (`effectType`, `scorePerTick`; binding `types/.../season4/ScoreFlag.kt`, module `arena/season_4/pain_and_gain/basic/prototypes`), constants `TICKS_LIMIT`/`MAX_SCORE_PER_TICK`/`FLAG_TYPES` (`PainAndGainConstants.kt`), the debuffs arrive as `GameObject.effects` (`EFF_*_MODIFIER` with `data.multiplier`; the bot also derives them from flag ownership and logs both — they must agree in the log). **Design:** homes = the initial centroids of both armies (no spawns); weaponless/heal-less creeps are *runners* that capture flags by swing per tick of their own walking time, weighted by the flag's cost in power; a flag is captured only while the army *with* that debuff keeps ≥ `CAPTURE_FLOOR` (0.9) of the enemy's power — the marginal Lanchester price with both sides' effects (`powerAfter`: a ranged flag cheapens only ranged dps, a heal flag raises the enemy's net dps, vulnerability shrinks hits) — or whenever the score projection says we lose (`behindOnScore`, self-limiting: it flips back once our rate leads); the army moves as one group with a posture: ANNIHILATE (≥ `PUSH_RATIO` by effects-aware power, or *in contact* unless retreat is feasible — no enemy melee adjacent and our slowest mobile creep faster than their fastest), RETREAT (enemy ≥ `RETREAT_RATIO` and near, to home or an own-half corner, never across the map), FLAG (the best flag objective by swing×cost/travel, guarded ones by Lanchester + fight cost within the group's speed slack), HOLD (post at the centroid of our flags); inside: local aggression with hysteresis, cohesion among mobile weapon holders only (healers follow their charge and never hold; nobody holds within `ARRIVED_SLACK` of the target), focus fire by *threat per hit* (killable first, then (live melee + live ranged + heal)/hits — "healers first" lost a mirror fight 11:6, threat-per-hit won it 12:9), `InfluenceMap` multiplies dps/heal by the creeps' effects and incoming damage by our vulnerability. **Offline stub** (job tmp dir, same loader-hook harness as spawn-and-swamp, no spawns): a point-symmetric 100×100 map with seven flags and a mirrored 14-creep army (bodies guessed: 2×M4 runners, 4×T2M5A3, 4×M4R4, 2×M3H3, 2×M5R2), enemy scripts `none|grab|rush|greedy`, early end on annihilation or unreachable lead. v1 results: none 10864:0 (two side flags, the rest blocked by the floor), grab 24672:16542 with no losses, rush — enemy army destroyed at t=426 (12 killed for 9), greedy — destroyed at t=303; errors 0.

## Version history (one paragraph per live match; v1–v3 are folded into the design paragraph above)

**Match 1 (04.09.2026, won 10556:0 by unreachable lead against a bot that never moved a creep):** confirmed — 14 creeps: 2×`M1` scouts, 4×`M8A8` (body `A×8 M×8`: weapons FIRST, MOVE last, so damage strips dps before speed — the opposite of the spawn-and-swamp bodies), 5×`M6R6` (`R×6 M×6`), 3×`M6H6` (`H×6 M×6`); flags (49,49) D5 in the centre, (90,8)/(8,90) H4, (67,31)/(31,67) A3, (13,49)/(85,49) R3; homes (12,9) and (85,88); a 100×100 map with two big wall blocks (x 21–41 and 60–78, y 36–62) flanking the centre; `effects` is exposed on both sides' creeps (`[{effectType, endTime: 2010, data: {multiplier}}]`) and matches the flag count; a flag stays owned after the capturer leaves. **v2 fixes:** (1) runner assignment was a per-runner greedy pass in the order of current assignments, so the two scouts swapped the centre flag every tick and each oscillated on one cell for 1500 ticks — now a global greedy matching over (runner, flag) pairs by value; (2) `packAt` counted every enemy that *could* reach a flag before us, so a motionless enemy army "guarded" every flag and ours held a post for 1500 ticks at 7/tick — enemies that have not moved for `STILL_TICKS` (20) no longer count as arriving (guards within 11 cells still do); (3) fighters at the post swapped places hundreds of times per 100 ticks — `STAY_BIAS` (5) keeps a creep on its cell outside combat unless a neighbour is clearly better. Stub replay of the match-1 map (`MAP=map-match1.txt`, real bodies and start positions, enemy script `none`): three flags by t=88 (R3, then D5 by a scout at t=42, then H4), 12/tick, unreachable lead at t=1369; `rush` on that map — enemy destroyed at t=237 for one creep; `grab` — 24293:8223. Still open: the other four flags stay POISED behind the power floor while the enemy idles — to the floor a patient full-strength enemy and a dead bot look the same.

**Match 2 (04.09.2026, won on points ~11968:9587 at 2000 ticks; we were player 2 at (85,88) on a *different* 100×100 map — the arena has several maps, flag coordinates were identical):** the opponent moved only its two scouts (one took (13,49) at t=42 and died when our army came; the other stepped onto the centre D5 at t=113 while our army was away at (13,49) and sat there to the end). Our army froze at t≈120 twelve cells from the centre for the rest of the match and both runners stayed POISED at the A3 flags. Reproduced on the match-2 map (`MAP=map-match2.txt START=match2`, enemy script `scouts`): the separation penalty (4 per adjacent ally) plus the stay bias (5) exceeded the flow gain of one step (10) — in a column the cell ahead is adjacent to two allies — so nobody moved. **v3:** separation applies only inside the arrival zone (flow ≤ standoff + `ARRIVED_SLACK`) and in combat, the stay bias only when settled there; a runner values a flag the power floor forbids at a fifth and own flags are always eligible, so an idle runner sits on our most valuable flag and blocks enemy runners. The same map with the `grab` script (the enemy army camping on our side) exposed a piecemeal defeat 0:12: the army split over the two routes around the wall blocks (flow-lag cohesion cannot see a lateral split), the four that met the camp were sent at the nearest prey by ANNIHILATE and died while eight marched the other way. Now on the march to a flag a fighter farther than `RALLY_RANGE` (12, hysteresis to 6) from the vanguard (lowest flow, ties by id) walks to the vanguard and the vanguard waits for it (mates with period ≤ 3 only); non-enemy targets use a flow that treats cells within 4 of *stationary* combat enemies as walls (a moving enemy is met in formation — avoiding it scattered the post a tick before contact); in ANNIHILATE a creep whose local group is below 0.9 of the local enemy (0.5 once fighting) retreats to the army centroid instead of charging. The stub had a bug that masked a regression: per-part damage clamping made a 6-hit healer immortal under three melee (the engine sums damage and heal before the death check; overkill is now subtracted from heal). Results: match-2 map — scouts 14949:96 at 1407, grab 23787:3807 at 1201 with no loss (was 18861:9462 with five lost), rush destroyed at 332, none 14960:0; match-1 map — none 15776:0, rush destroyed at 178, grab 18860:4511, scouts 16251:4670; synthetic — rush destroyed at 217, greedy at 620 (321 before rally: the waits slow pursuit), grab 24522:10312, none 10864:0; errors 0. `USE_AVOID`/`USE_RALLY`/`USE_LOCAL_ANNIHILATE` are bisection switches for the stub, all true.

**Match 3 (04.09.2026, LOST by annihilation; a third map, we were player 2):** the first active opponent — its army took the centre D5 at t=39, its own A3 at t=62, hovered by its H4 corner, attacked when our army came within ~15–20 cells, focused one target until dead, kept its healers adjacent, and after the battle swept the flags and hunted our scouts. Six defects, all in the log: (1) *involuntary captures* — a flag is captured by whoever stands on its cell, so the army marching through the centre took D5 (t=78) and the second A3 (t=96) with `occupant=none`, and fought a clean enemy at 0.77 with A×0.6 R×0.8 D×1.1 (the enemy's own debuffs were the ones we had just relieved); (2) *the capture rule chose weakness* — `CAPTURE_FLOOR` 0.9 passed each flag separately while the sum reached 0.77, `powerAfter` priced only our side (taking their flag also restores their dps), and `behindOnScore` unlocked everything at 6:10; (3) *contact with one lone shooter flipped the whole army to ANNIHILATE* (t=60), then the 0.9 release ratio kept it marching to the enemy corner at 0.97 (t=60–96); (4) *healers hovered 2–4 cells from their charges and fled* (`flee=true` for all three from t=140) — 24 heal/tick by rangedHeal while the enemy healed 216 adjacent; (5) *the army chased a weaponless wreck* (`raider`, no strength check) straight into the enemy's main body (t=100); (6) *a scout walked into the enemy army* at their corner and "fled" without a step (t=80–87). **v4:** a flag not ours is an obstacle for everyone except the creep assigned to it (`Ctx.flagCells`/`flagBlocked` in flow fields, `bestSingleMove`, the runner path matrix and flee) — captures are decisions, never side effects; `powerAfter` prices both sides (`hypoMods`/`powerOf`), `CAPTURE_FLOOR` is 1.0 ("not weaker"), 0.95 against an army that has not moved for `PASSIVE_TICKS` (100; at exactly 1.0 a dead bot gives 0:0, at 0.85 a sleeping camp woke to find us at 0.83), and the last 300 ticks unlock captures for the side behind; being behind lowers the push ratio to `PUSH_RATIO_BEHIND` 1.1 instead of unlocking captures (the leader is the weaker side by construction — debuffs are global per side); ANNIHILATE splits into `pushing` (power ≥ 1.3, release 1.1) and a contact fight scoped to the pack within 11 cells with no hysteresis; RETREAT at 1.15 (release 1.05) decided before contact, and in contact the per-tick rule — fight while enemy melee is adjacent, otherwise fall back under fire — is kept deliberately: on the stub it kites a pursuit 10:0 where "fight to the end once caught" gave 2:11 and "retreat only" 2:6; a local group that holds half the army never "retreats to the centroid" (that retreat was a walk under blows for 140 ticks); healers score cells by flow to the charge with a light fire term (`HEALER_W_DAMAGE` 0.05 — two heavier/adjacent variants lost the sleeper fight 2:12 at all four wake-up times, this one won 10:0 at all four; the H×6 M×6 body loses 12 heal/tick per 100 damage for the rest of the match) and flee only with no armed ally within heal range; a raider is chased only if the strikers beat its escort by 1.3; runners skip flags whose pack arrives before them (`packAt`) and fall back to a greedy flee step when the path search returns nothing; a creep walking to the vanguard never holds (four rally-ers and a straggler waited for each other for 1600 ticks). Stub: `map-match3.txt`, the `army` script (D5 → own A3 → hover by the corner, engage within 20, healers adjacent, focus lowest hits, sweep after our army dies) and `sleeper` (idle until `SLEEP`=500, then rush). Results: match-3 map — army lead at 1418 (19471:4893, no losses, the scripted army never leaves its corner), rush destroyed at 298 with no loss, sleeper lead at 1268 (21487:3160, 11 alive vs 1), none 11520:0 at 1540; match-2 map — scouts 16112:8603 at 1700, grab 18735:9754 at 1642 with no loss, rush destroyed at 240 with no loss, none 11520:0; match-1 map — none 11520:0 at 1540, rush destroyed at 179 (13 alive), grab 23443:1833 at 1136, scouts 18909:14167 at 1811; synthetic — rush destroyed at 216 (8 alive), greedy at 439 (12), grab 24428:16702, none 6552:0 at 1738; errors 0, ghost 0. Passive-enemy scores are lower than v3 by design (two flags instead of three at 0.95). Open: against an idle army whose scouts take flags (m1 scouts) the win is narrow — the idle army stays just under the 1.1 push ratio; a real fight against an active army has not been seen live with v4.

**Match 4 (04.09.2026, LOST by annihilation at t≈432; a fourth map, we were player 2):** the opponent took D5 with its whole army at t=39 and marched straight at ours (contact at t=57, ratio 1.02 in our favour — they carried D×1.1). Three defects: (1) *piecemeal engagement* — the army arrived in a march column: melee_2 entered the enemy blob alone, the ranged were 4–5 cells behind and the healers farther, and the old cohesion release ("any mate in combat releases every hold") was in effect an order to charge one by one — two melee lost all ATTACK parts in six ticks, the exchange went 3:1 against us at equal power; (2) *the retreat scattered the army* — `retreatPoint` maximised distance from the enemy and picked the far corner (3,96) past the enemy's flank, then switched to (96,96) as the enemy centroid moved, and the avoiding flow (cells near *stationary* enemies are walls — and a fighting enemy is stationary) sent a pair around the wall block to the opposite edge; creeps ended in three corners and were hunted one at a time; (3) *first-tick CPU timeout* (`Error: Script execution timed out` in `wallBetween`): three separate 10 000-cell `getTerrainAt` scans (walls, swamp, the map log) crossed the isolate boundary 30 000 times on tick 1. **v5:** *formation before contact* — with combat enemies within 11 a fighter outside their fire (>3 cells from every combat enemy) does not advance until `FORM_SHARE` (0.75) of the armed mobile creeps within `RALLY_RANGE` of the vanguard (the armed creep nearest the enemy) stand within `FORM_RANGE` (3) of it; the vanguard and those already gathered hold, the rest walk to the vanguard with every cell within 3 of an enemy blocked; creeps already inside fire fight normally; *retreat* — the point is chosen once per retreat (kept while the enemy is farther from it than we are) by distance gained from the enemy per cell walked (home: +5, the far corner: −4), the retreat flow no longer avoids stationary enemies, and a fighter more than `RETREAT_GAP` (3) cells ahead of the rearmost armed mate on the retreat field waits for it while outside fire; *terrain* is read in one scan shared by walls, swamp, the map log and `passable`. Stub: `map-match4.txt`, `hunter` script (D5 with the whole army, then straight at ours — the match-4 opponent). Results (v5): match-4 map — hunter lead at 1076 with no losses (13 killed), rush destroyed at 296 (13 alive), army at 245 (13), sleeper at 609 (12), none 11520:0 at 1540; match-3 map — hunter destroyed at 268 with no loss, rush at 390 (13), sleeper at 697 (11; v4 had a points win with 11 vs 1), army lead at 1418, none at 1540; match-2 map — scouts 16112:8603 at 1700, grab 18735:9754, rush destroyed at 348 with no loss, none 11520:0; match-1 map — rush destroyed at 250 with no loss, grab at 241 (13), scouts 18909:14167 at 1811, none 11520:0; synthetic — rush at 217 (8), greedy at 208 (6 alive, was 12 — guessed bodies, not investigated), grab 24428:16702, none 6552:0; errors 0, ghost 0. At contact in the hunter replay all nine fighters stand within six cells of each other. Open: the scripted enemies charge into our formation; a live opponent that also forms up has not been seen.

**Match 5 (04.09.2026, LOST by annihilation at t≈517; a fifth map):** the opponent split — its four melee plus one healer came at us as bait (contact t=69, ratio 1.0; it took no flag, not even D5 it stood on for twenty ticks), and its five ranged plus two healers roamed as a kiting body. We fought the bait well (t=70–163: 4 M8A8 + 1 M6H6 killed for one healer's HEAL parts) but *chased* it across the whole map to the NW corner for 80 ticks, then hunted the kiting body for another 140 (pushing at 1.66, prey = nearest enemy, the enemy backing off at equal speed), then two melee chased a lone healer forty cells to the SW corner while the other half of the army was caught alone by five ranged + two healers (t=387–397); the retreat then chose the corner (3,96) ("+20 net gain" vs home 76 cells away), and in the corner the RETREAT posture kept everyone non-aggressive with nowhere to step — seven creeps shot to pieces from three cells without striking back. Root defect: the army pursued enemies that move away at equal speed and can never be caught. **v6:** *catchability* — an enemy is `catchable` when within 2 of an armed creep, slower on plain than our fastest, or not `evasive` (over `CHASE_WINDOW` = 8 ticks it has not moved more than one cell away from where our armed centroid *was* — a kiter holds its distance to us, so distance alone never exposes it; its movement away from our old position does); only catchable enemies are prey, engage targets, hunt threats or chased raiders, and `pushing` needs at least one; ANNIHILATE with no catchable prey (or no local superiority) huddles at the armed centroid and shoots whatever comes within range; a *leash* (`LEASH_RANGE` 8) pulls any fighter with combat enemies within 11 back to the armed centroid; *retreat* goes home unless the enemy centroid is closer to home than we are (only then a corner by net gain), and an army that has arrived at its retreat point with the enemy still in contact fights (retreat is not feasible from a corner). Stub: `map-match5.txt`, `kite` script (melee + one healer as bait that fights 40 ticks and runs for the corner farthest from us; ranged + healers back off from any fighter within 2, close to range 3 otherwise, sweep flags when nobody is within 12). Results (v6): match-5 map — kite destroyed at 421 (12 alive), hunter at 154 with no loss, rush lead at 1094 (13 vs 1), army lead at 1416, none 11520:0; match-4 — kite at 475 (9), hunter lead at 1076, rush at 326 (13), sleeper at 609 (12); match-3 — kite at 413 (12), rush at 251 (13), sleeper at 697 (11), army lead at 1416; match-2 — rush at 220 with no loss, scouts 16112:8603, grab 18735:9754; match-1 — rush at 250 with no loss, grab at 241 (13), scouts 18909:14167; synthetic — rush at 217 (8), greedy at 357 (8), grab 24428:16702; errors 0, ghost 0. Open: the stub kiter walks into range 3 of our blob by itself and dies to it; the live opponent stayed out of reach and only engaged stragglers — against it the win must come from flags while the blob refuses the chase, which no live match has shown yet.

**Match 6 (04.09.2026, LOST by annihilation at t≈405; a sixth map, we were player 1 at (12,9)):** the opponent came at us with its whole army, ranged in front, and won the first exchange 1:10 at ratio 1.0 (t=64–80). Four defects: (1) *flight from a blob* — the lethal-damage rule (`hits < netDamageAt × 2`) counts every enemy that can reach a cell, which next to a twelve-creep blob exceeds any creep's hits, so the front melee turned their backs at the first tick of contact and were cut down from behind (the stub shows three full-health melee with `flee=true` at t=550 of a sleeper run); (2) *retreat at the first loss* — losing one melee's ATTACK parts reads as 1.18, above `RETREAT_RATIO` 1.15, so RETREAT started at t=69 while in contact, the ranged held for the rear without firing and ANNIHILATE/RETREAT then flipped every tick (t=69–100); (3) *a scout as bait* — an enemy scout twelve cells ahead of its army passed the raider gate (escort = combat enemies within 11), and the army left home to meet it at t=50, straight into the whole enemy army; (4) *mass attack against healers* — `rangedMassAttack` was chosen whenever the rate sum exceeded one part, spreading damage over three to five targets that three healers (216/tick) healed back, while the enemy focused 540/tick on one of ours (sleeper stub: our net fire ≈200/tick). **v7:** an armed creep flees only when being finished (lost half its remaining hits last tick and under a third left); a contact fight is left only at `RETREAT_CONTACT_RATIO` 1.5; a raider's escort is `packAt` (every combat enemy reaching it no later than we do); `FORM_RANGE` 2 with `FORM_PATIENCE` 10 (after ten ticks of waiting the formation advances with whoever came — otherwise readiness could never come at the fire edge and the army stood 1300 ticks four cells from a camp in ANNIHILATE, m1 grab); focus fire against a healing enemy (mass attack only at ≥2.5 rate); and a deficit on the score projection lasting `BEHIND_PATIENCE` 200 ticks is a stalemate — the push ratio drops to 1.0 (m3 army sat behind by one point a tick for 1500 ticks at 1.01). Stub: `map-match6.txt` (no START — we are player 1). Results (v7): match-6 — hunter destroyed at 186 (13), kite at 482 (13), rush lead at 1093 (13 vs 3), sleeper lead at 1275 (12 vs 2), none 11520:0; match-5 — kite at 613 (12), hunter at 233 with no loss, rush at 370 (12), army lead at 1419; match-4 — hunter at 263 no loss, kite at 556 no loss, rush at 143 (12), **sleeper lost (0 vs 11 at 725)**; match-3 — army at 414 (13; v6 needed until 1992 on points), kite at 441 no loss, rush at 230 (13), sleeper points win 17315:12874 with 2 alive vs 10; match-2 — rush at 303 (13), scouts and grab unchanged; match-1 — grab at 293 (13), rush at 331 (12), scouts 18909:14167; errors 0, ghost 0. Open: the `sleeper` runs — our army at R×0.6 H×0.75 from the passive-floor captures against a full rusher — are lost or nearly lost under the hold-the-line doctrine (v6 won them by the retreat/annihilate flicker that the same doctrine now forbids); the debuffed straight fight is ~0.93 in raw dps and we lose it 1:12, so our line fighting is still far below a dumb rusher's "everyone at the nearest"; a compact blob opponent that forms up has beaten every version so far and v7 has not met one live.

**Match 7 (03.09.2026, lost by annihilation at t≈270, the enemy lost nothing; player 2, a seventh map):** the log opened with `Script execution timed out` at tick 1 again — this time in `drawDebug` (the influence overlay: ~57 000 `contribution` calls per tick, 13×13 cells × 12 shooters × 28 creeps) — so tick 1's plan was lost; the first exchange went 0:2 because the ranged walked to the vanguard with fire cells blocked (`formGo`) and never fired while the melee vanguard fought alone three to five cells ahead; and at 1.53 RETREAT came on inside contact and ANNIHILATE/RETREAT flickered every tick until the army was gone — the pattern of matches 4 and 6. **v8:** `DEBUG_VISUALS=false` (the log carries every number the analysis needs); *compactness* — with an enemy in reach a creep may step only onto a cell within `COMPACT_RANGE` (2) of the armed centroid or with two armed mates adjacent, so the vanguard does not step at the enemy alone and the line advances as a line (the `formGo` fire-cell block is gone); *no retreat from contact* — `retreatFeasible = !contact && !atRetreatPoint` (at equal speed contact cannot be left, and the flicker handed the army over one creep at a time); and a *grabber* (a fighter sent to pick up a flag beside the route) neither holds for cohesion nor is waited for — it held at its flag for the group while the group held for it as a laggard on the way to the objective, 1200 ticks with two enemy scouts sitting on our flags (m3 kite: points loss 9128:23661 → enemy destroyed at 424 with no loss). Stub: `map-match7.txt`. Results (v8): match-7 — hunter destroyed at 299 (13 alive), kite at 323 (14), rush at 335 (9), sleeper at 795 (10), none 11520:0; match-6 — hunter 314 (11), kite 597 (13), rush 194 (12), sleeper lead at 1284 (10 vs 2); match-5 — kite 501 (14), hunter 291 (12), rush 320 (14), army lead 1419; match-4 — hunter 275 (12), kite 385 (14), rush lead 1531 (11 vs 3), sleeper points win 17356:12858 (2 vs 10); match-3 — kite 424 (14), army 414 (12), rush 324 (11), sleeper points win 16105:8645 (2 vs 10), none 11520:0; match-2 — scouts 16112:8603, grab 18731:9754, rush 334 (12); match-1 — grab 260 (13), rush 281 (11), scouts 18909:14167; synthetic — rush 259 (5), greedy 313 (10), grab 24428:16702; errors 0, ghost 0. Open: the `sleeper` runs at R×0.6 H×0.75 (m3, m4) are still won only on points with two survivors; v8 has not met a live compact-blob opponent.

**Match 8 (04.09.2026, lost by annihilation at t≈250, the enemy lost five creeps, its four melee among them; player 2, an eighth map):** the opponent took D5 with its whole army at t=39 and marched straight at ours (the match-4 pattern); contact at t=59 at 1.02 (they carried D×1.1, we R×0.8 from our R3). The melee exchange was even — all four enemy melee dead or weaponless by t=72 for three of ours — and the ranged phase was lost outright: our three healers stood 4–5 cells behind the line from t=59 to t=90 with `step=stay` and healed nothing while the enemy's 216/tick restored everything we hit. Three mechanisms: (1) the v8 compactness rule was a trap — a creep outside range 2 of the armed centroid could not step anywhere (its neighbours are outside too), so a healer that trailed by three cells at contact was frozen for the whole fight; (2) healer scoring stalled short of the charge — the arrival-zone separation penalty (4 per adjacent ally against a flow gain of 10) held a healer at 3–4 cells out of combat, and the fire weight 0.05 (a five-shooter cell costs 15) held it at the fire edge in combat; (3) a fighter that lost its weapons became a runner and walked off to flags — while the log shows the engine restoring parts through healing (parts live by total hits: damage strips them from the front, healing restores them from the back — melee_1 went from M5 416 to M8A8 by t=199 next to one healer), so a weaponless fighter next to a healer is a fighter again in six ticks. **v9:** *wounded* — a fighter with dead weapons stays in the army while a mobile healer lives (`wounded`, switch `USE_WOUNDED`), walks to the nearest healer (standoff 1, fire-averse, `WOUNDED_PRIORITY` lowest in traffic), never counts as armed (`combatArmy` for focus/contact/local groups, `weightedHits` 0) and becomes a runner only when no healer is left; *healers* have no separation penalty and, while their charge is engaged, a fire weight that only breaks ties between cells of equal closeness (`HEALER_W_DAMAGE_FIGHT` 0.005, switch `USE_HEALER_ADJ`); the charge is the most damaged armed fighter within 4 in combat and the most damaged patient (armed or wounded) out of it; a healer follows its charge in RETREAT too, and a healer or wounded creep flees only with nobody of the army within 3; *compactness without traps* — a step closer to the armed centroid is always open (switch `USE_COMPACT_CLOSE`) and a step *forward* along the flow is open up to `COMPACT_RANGE + 1`, so the line advances as a caterpillar (without it the blob stood 1200 ticks four cells from the enemy's last healer and lost on points — m5 kite); *the front row is for the armed* — a healer or wounded creep never steps onto a cell adjacent to a combat enemy (three healers took the row in front of their own melee — m7 sleeper), and a healer may push a wounded creep out of its way (traffic swap; letting fighters push healers made every fight worse); *no strikers* — a group of healers and wounded may leave contact (`retreatFeasible` with `strikers.isEmpty()`). Bisection on the stub: each of the three switches alone flipped the m7 sleeper grind, whose v8 "win" was ten scattered wrecks outliving a spent enemy; with the front-row rule and the caterpillar it is a real win. Stub: `map-match8.txt`. **Match 9 (04.09.2026, lost by annihilation; a ninth map, player 2):** the opponent walked its whole army straight at our post (no flag on the way, its scouts never moved), contact at t=119 at 1.0, and the exchange was lost outright by t=140. The melee traded evenly (three of ours disarmed for two of theirs by t=128) and the healers decided it: the enemy shot ours first — the v9 healers stood adjacent to their charge on the enemy's side of the blob (`HEALER_W_DAMAGE_FIGHT` 0.005 broke ties only), healer_1 had no HEAL part by t=130 and healer_2 by t=140 — while the enemy's three healers stayed two to four cells behind its line, took no damage in the whole fight, and afterwards restored four wrecks to full bodies within fifty ticks (M8 → M8A8 by t=185); our wrecks, with no healer left, became runners and were hunted down. A scout captured our R3 at t=125, in the middle of the exchange, for R×0.8 on the decisive fight. **v10:** *healers out of reach* — a healer's (and a wounded creep's) cell adjacent to a live enemy melee costs `HEALER_W_MELEE` (25, two and a half flow steps — a step away once a melee has come up; it never steps next to an enemy anyway), and the fight fire weight is `HEALER_W_DAMAGE_FIGHT` 0.02, so among the cells next to its charge the healer takes the one with the least fire and a cell under the fire of five shooters loses to a step back — 0.005 was a tie-breaker that let it walk into the front row, 0.05 kept it at the fire edge in every fight and lost the grinds by rangedHeal alone; two stricter rules were tried and dropped — 'no cell within two of an enemy melee' (with a melee on the far side of the charge every adjacent cell is within two, the healer retreated to four cells, out of even ranged heal, and a rush at 3967:3783 became a rout on m2) and a centroid-based 'not in front of the charge' (in a scrum every cell next to the charge is in front of something); *enemy healers are not a focus target by value* — `HEALER_VALUE` 3 (a dead healer being the only permanent loss) was tried and turned the m2 rush, won without a loss, into a rout: the live-like healers stand behind their line and heal each other, and our fire went into them while their front cut ours down — it stays at 1, a healer is shot when it is killable; *no captures in contact* — `captureAllowed` refuses any flag while the army has strikers and is in contact with a threatening enemy (the last-call unlock stays; with no strikers left the points are all that remains); *a pack without damage is no fight* — `threatening` is an enemy with live melee or ranged parts, or a healer with an enemy that has weapon parts in its body, alive or dead, within heal range plus one (such a healer restores it in six ticks); posture contact, retreat, hunting, the huntable prey and the pack guarding a flag count only threatening enemies: two surviving enemy healers kiting four cells from our blob kept the army in ANNIHILATE for 1700 ticks, chasing and huddling by turns while the enemy scouts took five flags (m7 rush, a points loss), and three healers healing each other made the fight cost of every flag infinite for a debuffed army that then held its post for 1500 ticks with six flags free (m2 rush) — ignoring healers outright was wrong too: the army walked away from healers with wrecks beside them, and the wrecks came back as full bodies. *The landing gate* skips the `sleeper` grinds (`grind` in `regress.sh`): the debuffed fight after passive captures is lost by construction and its points outcome depends on where the enemy wanders afterwards — two identical fights on m4 ended 17229:12409 and 12127:19175 — they are still run and reported in full mode. Stub: `map-match9.txt` and the `nine` opponent (straight at our army as one blob, charging from six cells; our healers shot first, mass attack never; its healers two cells behind their line, stepping away from our armed creeps within two) — the stub does not reproduce the live loss (v9 already wins it: the approach geometry there leaves our healers behind the line), it guards the mechanism; and the `rush`/`sleeper` healers now keep two cells behind their line like the live opponents (glued to the front they made the stub's rush stronger than any live army and rewarded front-row healers on our side: v9 won the debuffed sleeper grinds outright only because the rush never shot its healers). **Match 10 (04.09.2026, lost by annihilation at t≈175; a tenth map, player 1 at (12,9)):** the match-9 opponent again — the whole army straight at our post from tick 1, scouts never moving — and the same script: contact at t=71 at 1.0, an even trade of weapons for twenty ticks (by t=90 both sides had almost no damage left), then their three healers, two to four cells behind their line and untouched all fight, restored their wrecks to M8A8 while ours were shot down (healer_1 without HEAL by t=85, healer_3 by t=112, healer_2 by t=126 — healer_2 stood between two enemy melee at (14,10) with every neighbour blocked as 'front row'); at t=126 the survivors retreated to the corner (3,3) with the enemy at home, and were killed there. Four straight losses in an even fight at the post (matches 7–10). **v11 — do not fight an even army:** *EVADE posture* — when a threatening army we cannot push (`ours < theirs × pushRatio`) has come within `EVADE_RANGE` (25) of our centroid and we are not yet in contact, the army walks to the *evade point*: among the seven flags, both homes and the four corners, the point our strikers reach ahead of every enemy by the largest margin (their walk by their own bodies along the same flow), never the point we stand on (`EVADE_ARRIVED` 3), kept while within `EVADE_HYSTERESIS` (4) of the best, re-evaluated every `EVADE_EVAL_EVERY` (5) ticks or on arrival; a margin under `EVADE_MIN_MARGIN` (3) means cornered — the old RETREAT/contact rules apply. At equal speed a blob cannot be caught, so the pursuer either gives up or walks past flags it never takes; EVADE behaves like RETREAT for hunting, leash, formation, compactness and the rear hold, and fights back only in contact with local superiority. *Captures follow the doctrine* — a side that will not fight scores instead: `captureAllowed` returns true whenever our power is below theirs × `PUSH_RATIO` (the 'not weaker' floor protected a fight that is no longer our choice; the floor still applies when we are strong enough to push, and captures stay frozen while a striker is in contact); the army itself now takes flag objectives from tick 1. *Focus by kill time* — the focus target is the killable-this-tick enemy first, then the one with the largest threat removed per tick of fire (`threatOf` / `killTicks`, the latter from our fire in range minus the enemy heal on the target, adjacent heal in full and ranged heal at a third), then threat per hit; with this `HEALER_VALUE` 3 is back — a healer that is killable ranks high, one healed faster than we shoot has kill time infinity and ranks last (the value-only rule of the v10 experiment sent every shot into such healers). *A surrounded healer may step* — the front-row block applies only while the healer is not itself adjacent to an enemy. *An engage target is a threatening enemy, and an engaging creep neither holds for cohesion nor is waited for* — a fighter 'engaging' a lone enemy healer eight cells away held for its group while the group held for it as a laggard on the way to D5, 1500 ticks with the score running 6:8 against us (m1 rush); `engagingIds` carries the previous tick's engagers into the mates loop. *Cohesion patience* — a creep that has held for cohesion for `COHESION_PATIENCE` (30) ticks stops holding until the lag it waited for clears: the fourth mutual wait — a group cut in two by the centre swamp band and a wall block, one fighter crawling through swamp into a cell held by a mate who waited for a far mate who waited for the crawler — stood 1500 ticks at 10:11 on points (m9 kite); the wait rules cannot see who blocks whom, a clock can. **Match 11 (04.09.2026, army lost by t≈207, outcome on points unknown — the log ends at t=510 with us ahead 4957:3562 at 12:10 a tick, our last scout hunted and the enemy army sweeping our flags; an eleventh map, player 1):** the first live test of the evade doctrine, and its first defect. The opening was right — the army took R3 at t=40, the scouts D5 at t=44 and A3 at t=96, the army H4 at t=90, 15 a tick against zero while the enemy walked its whole army at us from 80 cells — but the H4 objective (8,90) was a 52-tick march into the south-west corner while the enemy closed from 80 cells to 40, the post (the centroid of our flags) landed inside a wall block so the army stood at the corner with `flow=-1` from t=90, EVADE at t=103 chose the same corner flag (arrival margin 15, no exit), the enemy closed the pocket, and at t=121 RETREAT led through it: contact at 0.81 with four debuffs, the army annihilated by t=207 with the enemy losing one creep. **v12 — a point without an exit is a pocket:** *escape candidates* are the seven flags and both homes (corners are gone: a corner with the enemy at its mouth is death); every candidate has two margins — *arrival* (the enemy's walk to it minus ours) and *exit* — and its score is the smaller. *The exit margin* (`exitMargin`) models the pursuer as walking **after us**, not to the exits: the enemy nearest to the point is projected down the flow to it (`projectAlong`) for `approachRate` × our arrival ticks (`approachRate` is the centroid distance change per tick over `APPROACH_WINDOW` = 20, clamped to 0..1: an idle army gets no head start, one marching at us a full one), and from that cell its walk to every other candidate minus our path onward is the exit, the best of them the margin; the flows are read next to a foreign flag (`flowNear`) because the flag cell itself is an obstacle in every flow field. Two rejected drafts, each found on the stub: a full head start to every exit refused every objective against an idle enemy and the army sat at home all match (m1 scouts); a head start scaled by approach still refused them against a marching one, and the army sat at home until the fight came to it (m10 hunter, m8 army). *The army's flag objective* needs an exit of at least `ESCAPE_MARGIN` (10) after arrival whenever an army we cannot push exists (`escapeNeeded`). *EVADE at any distance*, with the order set by the enemy's distance: with a combat enemy within `EVADE_RANGE` (25) of our centroid evade goes first and a flag objective is taken only when no escape point qualifies (with objectives first the army at five debuffs — 0.72 — flipped between objectives and escape points in different directions every 3–6 ticks, stood still in sum and was caught: m8 army lost 0:11); farther away the order is objective with exit → evade → retreat, the army holds where it stands only while that place scores at least `EVADE_SAFE` (20) and otherwise walks to the best-scoring point; the escape flows, enemy walks and nearest enemies are refreshed every `EVADE_EVAL_EVERY` ticks (nine fields) and shared by both decisions. *The post is a passable cell* (`passableNear`). *Two more defects found on the stub while replaying the match-11 map against the sleeper (the enemy that sits at home until t=500, then hunts):* **the retreat hold measured from the head** — the front waited whenever the rearmost armed creep was more than `RETREAT_GAP` (3) behind it, but a blob of twelve is 3–4 deep along its path, so the head stopped every other tick, a stopped head blocks the column behind it (`bestSingleMove` sidesteps around a static occupant), the tail fell further behind, and the escape ran at 0.5 cells a tick against a pursuer at 1.0 — caught in 46 ticks from a 13-tick margin; now the straggler is measured against the body, the median flow of the armed, and the front half waits only when the rear is more than `RETREAT_GAP` behind that median (0.85–0.9 cells a tick); **the evade pendulum** — a point we had reached and found scoring below `EVADE_SAFE` was excluded only while we stood within `EVADE_ARRIVED` of it, so two steps toward the next point it was a candidate again, outscored that point (6 against home's 4) and the army turned back, three swings in 13 ticks; the point we leave (`evadeLeft`) is not a destination until we have arrived somewhere else. *The escape is traced:* `evade:` lines print every change of the escape point with its arrival and exit margins, and the stub prints per-tick positions with `TRACE=from-to`. **Open finding — the chase itself is lost:** on every one of the eight sleeper maps, on v12 and on v11 alike, the army holding all seven flags (power 0.65 of the enemy's) is caught 60–120 ticks after the sleeper wakes and annihilated within the next hundred: the stub's pursuer moves at 1.0 with no traffic of its own, our blob at 0.85–0.9, every margin melts, the candidates left are behind the pursuer or pockets (from R3 on map 11: home with exit −8, everything else with negative arrival), the army walks to the last one, holds there and is caught, and contact at 0.65 is a lost fight by construction (`contactFight` turns the whole army on the vanguard, and the enemy's stream arrives two cells apart). The match is still won on points when the lead built before t=500 outlasts the enemy's remaining scoring (six maps of eight); on maps 6 and 7 the scouts died too and the stub counts a loss. What would remove it, for the operator to decide: an escape that runs a loop around a wall block instead of walking to points (an equal-speed pursuer cannot cut across an obstacle, so the lead is kept indefinitely), and a debuff budget that keeps the army able to break a vanguard while a full-strength army still lives; a live opponent's blob has its own traffic (match 11's came at 0.8), so the stub's hunter is the pessimistic case. The sleeper grinds stay outside the landing gate. Stub: `map-match11.txt` (player 1). Results (v12): match-11 — nine lead 23521:1514 at 1120, hunter destroyed at 189, kite destroyed at 337, rush lead 23596:1354 at 1111, sleeper lead 23715:834 at 1085; match-10 — nine lead 23670:1244 at 1104, hunter destroyed at 191, kite destroyed at 258, rush destroyed at 180, sleeper lead 23786:1334 at 1102; match-9 — nine lead 19564:4681 at 1405, hunter destroyed at 302, kite destroyed at 359, rush destroyed at 234, sleeper lead 23863:1196 at 1095; match-8 — hunter destroyed at 443, kite destroyed at 417, rush lead 23587:1691 at 1125, sleeper lead 23854:2927 at 1163, army lead 21808:4318 at 1302, none lead 23824:0 at 1049; match-7 — hunter destroyed at 219, kite destroyed at 406, rush destroyed at 220, **sleeper LOST at 805**; match-6 — hunter destroyed at 338, kite destroyed at 399, rush destroyed at 343, **sleeper LOST at 745**; match-5 — kite destroyed at 379, hunter destroyed at 303, rush lead 23784:994 at 1089, army lead 21724:4549 at 1314; match-4 — hunter destroyed at 208, kite destroyed at 359, rush lead 23488:1789 at 1134, sleeper lead 23855:2582 at 1150; match-3 — kite destroyed at 320, army lead 21383:8975 at 1504, rush destroyed at 210, sleeper lead 23857:102 at 1051, none lead 23806:0 at 1048; match-2 — scouts lead 23843:105 at 1051, grab destroyed at 212, rush lead 23293:1173 at 1117; match-1 — grab lead 21562:4597 at 1322, rush lead 23653:1203 at 1104, scouts lead 23674:418 at 1070; 2 failures, errors 0.

**Match 12 (04.09.2026, annihilated at t≈568 while ahead 7467:1474 — a twelfth map, player 2; the match-9 opponent again, a tight blob at 1.0 that ignores flags and hunts):** the opening took six flags by t=149 (R3 by a scout at t=42, D5 at 107, H4 (90,8) at 117, A3 (31,67) at 125, H4 (8,90) at 149) and the seventh, R3 (13,49), at t=190 — 19 a tick against zero — while the enemy toured D5, our half and its H4 corner and then, from t≈150, marched straight at us. Four defects. *The west-edge pocket:* at t=125 and t=149 the objectives H4 (8,90) and R3 (13,49) were 28- and 41-tick marches along the west edge with the enemy idle (approach 0), so the exit test held for a static pursuer; when the enemy marched, the reaction came 20 ticks later (`APPROACH_WINDOW`), the army was deep in the corridor with the enemy cutting it from the east, and every candidate was behind the pursuer or a pocket. *The abandoned point recaptured:* at t=188 R3 (13,49) had just been left (`evadeLeft`, score below `EVADE_SAFE`) and was chosen as the objective six ticks away, toward the enemy. *The corner fallback:* at t=190, with no escape point scoring above 3, RETREAT aimed the army at the corner (3,96) for a tick. *The scouts died:* scout 1 fled deeper into the H4 corner at t=117 — the flee goal was any cell 3 away from the enemies, and the nearest such cell lay deeper in the pocket — and was caught at its mouth; scout 2 stood at the west wall (4,40) for thirty ticks next to the fight (`greedyFlee` returned nothing because no neighbour beat its cell) and died; with both gone, the annihilation at t=568 ended a match we led by six thousand. Contact came at t=203 at 0.76 and the fight was lost 12:1. **v13:** *the pursuer projection carries the reaction lag* — in `exitMargin` the pursuer is projected `approachRate` × arrival **plus `REACTION_LAG`** (`APPROACH_WINDOW` + `EVADE_EVAL_EVERY` = 25) steps toward the point: an idle enemy can start walking at any moment and we learn it that late; the full worst case (the pursuer starts with us at 1.0) refused even the central D5 (exit 6 from every side) and the army sat at home all match (m1 scouts, lost on points 10743:14487); with the lag, D5 against a far idle enemy passes (16), the corner H4 of match 12 fails (0) and the west-edge R3 fails (−5). *The objective never targets the point just left* (`evadeLeft`). *Evade always yields the best candidate* — `EVADE_MIN_MARGIN` is gone: the worst of the candidates is still farther from the enemy than a corner, and the RETREAT fallback walked into the home pocket on the stub's m11 sleeper. *Scouts flee early and far:* from a threatening enemy within `SCOUT_FLEE_TRIGGER` (8), along a path to a cell beyond `SCOUT_FLEE_RANGE` (12) from every enemy (out of a pocket through its mouth, not deeper in), and in danger a step is always taken, to the best neighbour even when it does not beat staying (`greedyFlee(force)`): a scout is the insurance against annihilation — an empty MOVE never tires and cannot be caught on open ground. Stub: `map-match12.txt` (player 2) and the `twelve` script (D5, a loop through our half up to its H4 corner, then the match-9 hunt); the replay destroys the enemy at t=339 with 12 alive; the sleeper grind on map 12 is still lost by the army (2 alive, a points win) — the open finding above stands. **Match 13 (05.09.2026, annihilated at t=545 while ahead 5235:734 — a thirteenth map, player 1; the match-9 hunter again, marching at us from tick 1):** the operator's ruling after this match, and the doctrine from here on — **losing every creep loses the match whatever the score, so flags are not hoarded to run away on points; the army keeps a small lead with the armies roughly equal.** The match: one flag (R3 by a scout at t=43, power 0.97), the army back home by t=80, a lone enemy melee reached it at t=89 — contact, ANNIHILATE — and the *contact pack* (enemies within 11 cells of any of ours) took in the enemy's main body ten cells south, so the army walked off home into it and fought at 0.97 from t=110. The fight was lost 12:1 by an asymmetry the stub does not reproduce: the enemy's healers stayed two to four cells behind their line and were never touched, and once nine of their creeps had lost every weapon part those two healers regrew them (a part per 1.4 ticks each); our healers went to the front row to touch-heal the wounded melee and were shot apart by t=140 (their focus is healers first), so our disarmed creeps stayed disarmed. With no healer alive, `wounded()` is false by construction and eight disarmed creeps became runners: they captured A3, H4 (90,8) and R3 (85,49) — 18 a tick — and were hunted one by one on the corner flags (the last at (7,92) at t=545); the corner-flag runner fled at eight cells along the top edge and was intercepted diagonally. **v14 — parity:** *captures* — a flag is taken only while, after it, we keep at least `PARITY_FLOOR` of the enemy's power when we are not ahead or the lead is not growing, and only while we stay no weaker (`CAPTURE_FLOOR`) when we are; the floor is 0.97, the price of exactly one cheap flag from equality (R3 and A3 cost 3%, D5 and H4 4–5% and are taken only against a weakened enemy): 0.95 let two flags through, and the fight at 0.93–0.95 was a 12:0 rout where the same fight at 1.02 was a 12:0 win (stub m9 hunter, m5 rush) — nothing in this game is as steep as the power ratio at contact; *captures are budgeted per tick* (`plannedCaptures`): two captures in one tick — D5 by the army and H4 by a scout — each passed the floor alone and together gave 0.93, so the second capture of a tick now sees the first; *the passive floor is gone* (`CAPTURE_FLOOR_PASSIVE`, 0.95 against an army that has not moved): a sleeping army is still an army — it let a third flag through against the stub's m6 sleeper, which then woke and won the fight at 0.96; the v11 clause 'not strong enough to push — take everything' is gone (it gave seven flags at 0.65–0.76 and three annihilations in a row); *evasion* (`hunted`) is reserved for an army clearly stronger than ours — theirs at least `RETREAT_RATIO` (1.15) × ours — at parity the army holds its post and fights, since running from an equal army at equal speed only means being caught with the tail stretched (matches 11–12); *the contact pack* is the enemy within `RANGED_RANGE` + 2 of any of ours, not eleven cells; *runners under a hunter* (the escape flows are kept whenever an army we cannot push exists, strikers or not) skip a flag whose exit margin is negative and leave a flag when its exit closes, to the best escape point on their own path (`runnerEscape`); *the line holds when the enemy is near* — with an armed enemy within `NEAR_RANGE` and no push, the army takes no flag objective (the army that went for a corner flag as the enemy closed was caught in a column on the march: stub m3 sleeper, t=529–540, both sides disarmed by t=572 and only theirs regrew); *the healer's fire weight in a fight* (`HEALER_W_DAMAGE_FIGHT`) was tried at 0.05 against the 0.02 of v10 and gave identical outcomes to the tick on eight stub fights — the fight is decided by the power ratio at contact, not by this weight — so it stays 0.02. **Open finding — the even fight:** the replay of this map against the `nine` script is a won fight (12 kills, one loss), so the exact healer asymmetry of the live fight is not reproduced by the stub; what would remove it is a healer formation that keeps our healers out of the enemy's ranged reach the way theirs are kept, and the disarmed regrowing behind the healers instead of walking to flags. The scouts were hunted afterwards, one in the H4 corner at t≈243, the other poised beside a flag it was not allowed to take. **v15 — fight the way they do:** *rotation* (`ROTATE_OUT` 0.5, `ROTATE_IN` 0.9, `rotatingIds`) — a fighter that has lost half its weapon parts, with a healer alive, walks to the nearest healer, firing on the way, and returns when nine tenths of them are back (touch-healing regrows a part every 1.4 ticks); the threshold is counted in weapon parts, not hits, because the weapons sit at the front of the body — an M8A8 at half its hits is already disarmed, and a first draft by hits never rotated anyone and lost the stub's m3 army exchange that v14 had won; *engage only in line* — an engage needs the formation ready and at least two armed mates within `FORM_RANGE`; *no spike into fire* — an armed creep under fire with fewer than two armed mates adjacent steps back to the armed centroid instead of forward. The log shows the v17 rows failing against the enemy's line: its ranged wing stopped three cells from our melee and shot, its melee hovered at two to four and did not step in; our melee held at the centroid (kiters are not chased), and our ranged, placed two cells behind the melee centroid, stood five to six from any target and never fired — the front was shot for free (16000→12472 against 16000→14950 by t=100), then the block pushed the disarmed melee back. **v18 — the ranged row follows the distance to the threat:** the ranged stand `RANGED_RANGE − d` cells behind the melee centroid, where `d` is the front's distance to the nearest threatening enemy — adjacent contact keeps them two behind (reaching one cell past our front, as the enemy's do), a line at three puts them in the melee's own row so they trade shots, the rear stays one cell further; a first draft that split the cases "enemy melee within three / none" and swapped the ranged and melee rows inverted the formation under a closing mob, when the case flips within a couple of ticks (stub m6 sleeper, army lost), so the rows never swap. **Flag keepers** (`KEEP_RANGE`, `updateKeepers`): a fighter standing on our flag with an enemy runner within ten cells and none of our runners on or assigned to the flag stays on it — out of the strike group, the rows and the objective — until the runner leaves, our runner relieves it or a threatening enemy comes within reach; an empty MOVE scout cannot be caught, it steps away from the fighter and back the moment he leaves, and the regression showed the army taking R3, walking off toward D5 forty ticks away, losing R3 four ticks later and flipping its objective between the two for 1800 ticks while the enemy scored double from five flags (stub m8 kite). **First tick:** the map dump (a hundred `println` calls on the JIT-cold first tick) now prints from the second tick as one string. **Stub:** `map-match17.txt`; **Match 18 (05.09.2026, annihilated at t≈443 while behind 225:1917 — an eighteenth map, player 2 from (85,88); the same hunter):** the fight at 0.97 at the post R3 (85,49) from t=68 was lost 12:0 by t=196, and a tick timed out again — this time the second tick, where v18 had moved the map dump (the first tick has a ten-times larger budget, the second does not). Two defects in the fight: *a keeper in the thick of it* — a ranged stood on the flag from t=68 to the end without firing, because the enemy's scouts hovered within ten cells of the flag while its army was six to nine away, and the keeper was only released by a threat within reach of the keeper; and *the rows measured from the melee centroid* — the enemy's line stood three cells from our front-most melee, the melee centroid lay one to two cells behind that creep, and the ranged row at `3 − d` from the centroid was again four to five from the line and silent. **v19:** the rows are anchored on the *front creep* — the armed creep nearest to a threat — and `d` is its distance to that threat (`planBlock`), so a line at three puts the ranged in the front creep's own row; a keeper is neither made nor kept while a threatening enemy is within `KEEP_RANGE` of the flag; the map dump is printed in four parts of twenty-five rows on ticks 3–6, one string each, with the creep marks captured on tick 1 so the stub still gets the start cells; the flow field for creep targets within a few cells (heal mate, healer, engage, prey, the centroid when alone or leashed) is a *bounded* BFS (`NEAR_FLOW`, `DistanceMap.flowFieldTo(maxDist)`) with the full field as the fallback when the creep is outside it, and a `bfs t=… max=…` line counts the flow fields per tick — the live timeouts in matches 16–18 all point at flow-field BFS in the thick of a fight; the count showed 21–24 fields a tick (seven flags for the objective choice, nine escape points, the creeps' targets), so a field to a fixed target now lives `FLOW_TTL` = 5 ticks — its obstacles (walls, foreign ramparts, spawns, immobile creeps, foreign flags) rarely change, and a change of that signature drops the whole cache; fields to creep targets live one tick; a signature change only marks the fields stale, and a per-tick budget (`BFS_BUDGET`, eight full fields, a bounded one counts a quarter) serves a stale field beyond it rather than recomputing, and gives a *new* target beyond it a bounded field marked stale, so the full one is computed next tick within the budget — the start of a fight (enemy cells for engage and prey, evade points, flags evicted from the cache during the fight) still cost 16–17 full fields in one tick before that; entries unused for sixty ticks are evicted. The peak cost is now 9–11 full-field units a tick against 21–24 before the cache; the fight outcomes are unchanged and the stub runs twice as fast. **Stub:** `map-match18.txt` (player 2, `START=match2`); on v18 the stub again beat all four scripts on this map (wing 14/2, block destroyed at 355), so the live loss stays unreproduced; with v19 all sixteen fight scenarios are won, ten with the enemy destroyed. **Match 19 (05.09.2026, lost on points ≈12 600:24 300 at t≈1530 with the enemy army annihilated but for one scout — a nineteenth map, player 2 from (85,88); a *different* opponent):** the enemy never fought as an army: from t=39 it spread over all seven flags, one to three creeps per flag, and farmed 25 a tick while we held one flag at 3. Its debuffs made it *weaker* (our 3967 against its 2954), so from t=58 our army sat in ANNIHILATE by push and chased "catchable" creeps twenty to forty cells away for three hundred ticks — they stepped back at every approach, equal speed, the posture flickered ANNIHILATE/FLAG, and no flag was taken, because ANNIHILATE voids the flag objective. The kills came late and piecemeal (the last armed enemy at t=1493); the last M1 with 28 hits sat beside our R3 for five hundred ticks while the army walked to flags — annihilation would have won regardless of the score. **The stub reproduces it:** the `spread` script (every enemy creep takes a flag of its own, two per flag, steps away from our armed creeps within six and comes back) beats v19 2529:24339 with our army intact. **v20 — a hunt is judged by its result:** *stall* (`STALL_TICKS`, `STALL_COOLDOWN`) — with prey within engage range and no *net* damage to the enemy over twenty ticks (a hit healed the same tick is no progress) the push, the contact fight, the line hold, the local engage and the no-capture-in-contact rule are lifted for three hundred ticks or until net damage, and the army goes for flags, which the spread must yield; a first draft gated the push by distance to the prey instead, and it switched the push off against an army that does fight when approached (stub m3 army, both armies stood to the end) — distance is the wrong criterion; *keepers stay against a picket* (`KEEP_PICKET`): up to two armed enemies within keep range no longer void a keeper — the spread guards each flag with a pair and retook a flag ten ticks after the army left; a keeper below half hits is released; *the sweep*: with no armed or healing enemy left and the score behind, the army hunts the remaining creeps regardless of catchability — annihilation is the only win left; *parity under stall* (`PARITY_FLOOR_STALLED` 0.93): against an army that has not struck for twenty ticks with prey in reach the capture floor is 0.93 instead of 0.97 — 0.97 forbade the fourth flag while the spread held four, three against four to the end at 12:13 a tick; the first blow restores 0.97. **This is an assumption on the operator's doctrine (v14), named here for a decision.** **Open finding:** on maps 18 and 19 the spread still wins on points (it starts with six flags, and even 0.93 caps us at three or four: the fourth R3 would put us at 0.87); the two scenarios are `grind` lines outside the landing gate, map 12's spread is in the gate. **Match 20 (05.09.2026, army destroyed by t≈197 — a twentieth map, player 1 from (12,9); the army-type opponent of matches 16–18):** the enemy took no flag and marched its whole army straight at our R3, contact at t=72. **v20's stall misfired at the worst moment:** at t=64, the tick the enemy vanguard entered eight cells, the stall fired — its twenty-tick window had been counting since the start of the match and "prey in reach" was checked only at the moment — and the army, standing in formation on R3, turned for H4 at (8,90), forty-four ticks away, with its back to the approaching army; the contact came in the turn, ranged and healers on the enemy's side, the fight at 0.97 was lost 0:12 by t=100 with a single enemy loss, a second stall at t=117 in the losing fight (only our hits were falling) lifted the contact fight, and the army was gone by t=197; the runners were hunted, and the log ends at t=520 with one scout alive at 1446:0 — the enemy never took a flag. **The stub reproduces the misfire:** `hunter` on map 20 with v20 fires the stall at t=68, at contact (the stub's hunter is beaten anyway; the live one is not — the gap of matches 16–18 remains open). **v21 — a stall is a picket that has stood there:** a stall needs at most `STALL_PICKET` (four) armed enemies within engage range for `STALL_TICKS` *consecutive* ticks and no net damage of at least `STALL_DAMAGE` (300 hits) *either way* over the window; only such damage cancels a stall — a first draft cancelled it on any dip, and the pass-by volley of a picket (60–180 hits, healed a tick later) cancelled a three-hundred-tick stall mid-march, the flag objective vanishing with it (stub m12 spread). With that, the spread is beaten on maps 12 and 18 (map 18's line moves into the gate); maps 19 and 20 are still lost on points and stay `grind`. **Match 21 (05.09.2026, army destroyed by t≈199 — a twenty-first map, player 2 from (85,88); the army opponent of matches 16–18 and 20):** the cleanest sample of that fight so far. No stall fired; the army stood in rows on R3 and the enemy came from the south-west, contact at t=68, and the fight at 0.97 was lost 0:12 by t=100 with two enemy melee disarmed and healed back. The log shows how: **the enemy fights as a ranged screen with a melee reserve** — five ranged in a line at exactly three cells from our front, four melee one to two cells behind them, healers six to eight cells back that close up to the line once the exchange is won. Our melee, targeting "the nearest catchable enemy within eight" — a screen ranged at three — stepped forward one at a time and landed adjacent to the reserve: −720 and −840 in a tick, dead in two (melee_4 t=74–76, melee_3 t=77–78, the other two by t=84). Our healers stood four to six cells behind the front the whole fight: the reach rule (v16) forbids a healer any cell within three of an enemy ranged, which in a contact fight is every cell of the rear row, so the rows' rear slots were thrown out and the melee died unhealed — while the enemy's healers stood at their line under our fire and were never focused. Only two or three of our ranged had a target at any tick (four to five cells from the screen behind our own melee) against all five of theirs. **v22:** *healers in a contact fight avoid only melee reach* (`meleeReachCells`, two cells around an enemy melee) — the ranged reach is ignored while `contact` holds, so the rear row stands one cell behind the front and heals at twelve a part; outside contact the full reach rule stays; *melee hold the line* (`MELEE_HOLD_RANGE`): in a contact fight without a push a melee engages only what has come within two cells and otherwise keeps its cell in the front row — the screen is not chased into the reserve; with a push (we hunt) the engage range is unchanged. A third change was tried and reverted: alone-in-fire counting allies within two cells instead of adjacent (the guess was that a line comes apart from its ends) — with it the melee no longer fell back under fire and dived into the enemy blob one at a time, three melee in eight ticks for one kill, and the gate scenario m5 army was lost (t=806); back on adjacency it is won at 415. **The stub does not reproduce the loss:** `wing` is the screen model and every version since v17 beats it; the live screen wins five fights out of five (16–18, 20, 21). What the stub lacks is measured here for the next model: the reserve's punishment of a lone melee, healers that close up after the exchange, and focus fire on one creep. The open finding stands, and v22 is reasoning from the log, verified only for not losing what the stub already won. **Match 22 (05.09.2026, army destroyed by t≈390, then lost on points — a twenty-second map, player 1 from (12,9); the army opponent, first live fight on v22):** a close fight for the first time. Contact at t=68 on R3; the enemy's first loss by t=110, and at t=120 we led on hits (11 982 against 11 786) with the healers one to three cells behind the melee instead of four to six. It was lost from t=140 on: by t=150 the enemy had five nearly intact ranged (1 058–1 200 hits) against our three, while its melee — our focus target every time it stood adjacent — were disarmed and healed back at its line; a disarmed M6 with 542 hits stood for twenty-five ticks within two to four cells of our ranged and was never shot (hulks are outside the focus pool while armed enemies remain) and grew back to M8A8. R3 itself went to an enemy melee at t=137 as our line drifted west — which handed *them* the ranged debuff. **v23 (measured on the stub):** *the rear row stands directly behind the front* — the old row "one behind the ranged" put the healers three cells from the melee when the enemy was adjacent (`back` = 2), healing four a part instead of twelve; the ranged row's cells are now marked taken so the two rows never collide; *in a contact fight a healer avoids only the cells adjacent to an enemy melee* — the two-cell ring of v22 was the rear row itself; *keepers are released with hysteresis* (`KEEP_RELEASE`, twenty cells): the spread's picket stepped back past ten, the keeper left, and the picket retook the flag (stub m18 spread, D5 and R3 lost at 815 and 846). **Two attempts to bring fire onto the healed-back hulk were rejected by the stub:** a "regrowth threat" (dead weapon parts at half weight in every target's threat) lost m9 hunter, m3 army and every sleeper grind; "a hulk finishable in two volleys ranks right after kill-this-tick" lost m3 army, the sleepers and m18 spread. Fire on the live threat is what every stub win rests on; the hulk on the enemy's healing stays an open finding. The spread is now beaten on maps 12, 18 and 19; 20 and 22 are still lost on points and stay `grind`. **Match 23 (05.09.2026, army destroyed by t≈437 — a twenty-third map, player 1 from (12,9); the army opponent again):** we led the whole first half — R3 by t=39, H4 in the bottom-left corner by t=90, 7 points a tick against 5, and our power 3967 against 3737. The army was lost to a march. At t=163, ahead on score with the lead growing (which lowers the capture floor), the objective became the far H4 at (90,8): `travel=83`, straight across the map, while the enemy army was walking home to its own corner. It turned around. At t=200 the enemy was close enough for the objective to be dropped, and the army was left strung out over fifteen cells — and then **the formation split it in two**: the vanguard is chosen as the creep *closest to the enemy*, which on a march is the one that has run farthest ahead, so half the army walked east to gather on it, into the enemy, while the other half walked west to the post. At t=211 that single leading melee was in contact alone and its contact put the whole army into ANNIHILATE; it fought their formed line by itself for ten ticks, and the rest arrived one at a time. The grind that followed lasted two hundred ticks and cost all twelve for four of theirs. **v24 — the army's mass decides, not a creep that has run off** (`MASS_RANGE`, eight cells, the leash distance that pulls it back): the formation vanguard is chosen only among creeps within that distance of the armed centroid, and the army-level `contact` is read from those creeps too, so one detached creep can neither be the rally point nor drag the army into a fight it is not formed for. **Tried and rejected:** refusing a flag whose travel exceeds the enemy's straight-line distance to our army while we are ahead with a growing lead. It prevents no stub loss and makes the bot passive whenever it leads — m23 rush 23279→21496, m22 wing 23180→21362, m17 wing 23638→21510, m7 sleeper 22462→20033. The column is fixed by the mass rule, not by giving up the objective. **Match 24 (05.09.2026, army destroyed by t≈219 for one of theirs — a twenty-fourth map, player 2 from (85,88); the army opponent):** R3 by t=41, H4 in our corner by t=93, seven points a tick against five, and the enemy walked straight into us; contact at t=102 with the powers even (3967 against 3996 — the enemy's own D5 vulnerability against our R3 and H4 debuffs). We were wiped fourteen to one. **The log names the reason: two of our four melee stood at full health through the whole fight.** v22's rule that a melee "holds the line" in a contact fight was written for the melee *in* the line, but it asked nothing about where the creep was: at t=120 melee_3 at (81,14) and melee_4 at (85,15) were four to six cells from the nearest enemy with `step=stay`, and at t=140 they were still untouched (`M8A8 1600`) while the two melee that were in contact and the ranged behind them were being killed one by one. Half our melee power never entered the fight. **v25:** a melee holds its cell only while an enemy is within `MELEE_HOLD_RANGE + 1` — that is, one step from its strike range. Farther away it closes on the fight as before, and the line forms where the fight is. On the stub every scenario still passes and several improve (m22 army thirteen alive instead of twelve, the sleeper grinds on maps 6 and 7 won thirty to seventy ticks earlier with one more creep alive). **Match 25 (05.09.2026, a points loss without a single blow struck — a twenty-fifth map, player 2 from (85,88); a dispersed opponent that never moved):** the enemy took the centre D5 at t=39 and had all seven flags by t=80, twenty-two points a tick against our three; we clawed back R3, H4 and D5 and drew level on rate (12 against 13) but never closed a deficit of three thousand. **Both armies ended the match at 16000/16000 hits: in 1820 ticks nobody hit anybody.** From t=830 to the end — nine hundred and ninety ticks — every ten-tick sample shows our twelve army creeps on exactly the same cells, the same centroid (46,34), the same flow values and the same `moves: free 650 chain 200 swap 150` per hundred ticks: ten creeps stepping every tick and the army not moving a cell, while `pushing=true` and the prey stood eleven cells away at (60,34) behind the wall block at x=53–56. **The bot has a detector for a stalled fight and none for a stalled march.** The v20 stall fires only on a picket within `ENGAGE_RANGE` (eight); at eleven cells nothing was ever counted, so `pushing` stayed on, and `pushing` is what suppresses the flag objective — the army chased what it could not reach instead of scoring. **v26:** a chase that does not move the armed mass is a stall too — if the centroid of the armed army has not shifted by a single cell for `COHESION_PATIENCE + STALL_TICKS` ticks (fifty: longer than the longest lawful standstill, the wait for laggards) while pushing, out of contact and with no net damage either way, the same cooldown fires and the army goes back to flags. In match 25 that would have fired at t≈880 and turned nine hundred dead ticks into captures at a moment when one more A3 was worth three points a tick against a deficit of 3358. The window is measured, not named: it is the sum of two constants already in the code. The stub gained the match-25 map and a `roost` script (the dispersal that never leaves its flag, this opponent exactly). **The stub does not reproduce the freeze** — `roost` and `spread` on map 25 are both won, and the new detector fired exactly once in 175 scenarios (m21 wing, t=442, with the outcome unchanged); the case for the fix rests on the live log, and its safety on every one of the 158 earlier scenarios ending exactly as it did under v25. Open finding from the new script: on map 18 `roost` the enemy army is destroyed by t=203 and the match still lost on points 17683:24331, because the sweep chases two uncatchable scouts for fifteen hundred ticks while four enemy flags stand — the same class of defect (chasing instead of scoring) that this match showed, and not yet fixed. **v27 (05.09.2026, the open findings, on the operator's instruction to fix them all): the chase target was picked by comparing fields of different quality, not distances.** The new `roost` script reproduced match 25's freeze in the stub at last — on map 18 the enemy army is destroyed by t=203 and the army then stands on the same cells until the match ends, losing 17683:24331. The trace names the cause: the prey target alternates *every tick* between the two surviving scouts, one at (13,49) with a flow distance of 22 and one at (90,8) at 60, so the army steps west, then east, then west again. **Each candidate's distance was measured with its own BFS field, and the fields are computed under a budget (`BFS_BUDGET`): past it a candidate gets a bounded (`NEAR_FLOW`) or stale field, and "we could not measure this one" was read as "infinitely far".** That is not a comparison of candidates, it is a comparison of field quality — and the same thing froze the live army of match 25 for 990 ticks. **The fix measures every candidate with one field from the army's centroid, in one tick, in the same units** — it also costs less than a field per candidate — and an unreachable candidate is no longer chased at all. Two gaps in v26's safety net closed with it: the march stall required combat enemies to exist (the sweep runs precisely when none do, so the net had never once fired there), and the sweep itself stood outside the stall, so a frozen finishing-off could not be switched off by anything. Stub: m18 roost turns from a points loss into the enemy army destroyed at t=351 and joins the gate; five more scenarios turn from a win on points into the enemy army destroyed (m16 nine, m17 wing, m19 block, m20 block, m22 block) and m24 kite is 99 ticks faster; four kills come 40-60 ticks later and m21 spread's margin drops by 1648, all still won. **The dispersed opponent on maps 22 and 23 stays unbeaten, and it is not for want of trying: five eliminations were attempted and every one was rejected by the stub.** The trace shows why the army never arrives — guards stand four to five cells from the marching column and never fire (both armies end at 16000/16000), the flag objective is created and cancelled every ten ticks as the pack around the flag flickers 1-4 with their twitching, and cells next to a guard carry a potential-damage penalty that pins a creep that is taking no damage at all. Rejected, with numbers: "enemy near = near the army's mass, not any one creep" (m22 unchanged, m20 spread win -> loss, m19 block destroyed@389 -> lead@1142); "keepers out of the capture-contact test" (no change on any of the 175 scenarios); "a stall makes the fight-cost gate moot" (m22 and m23 won, m12 and m21 spread lost instead, m19 block destroyed -> lead); "the fight cost is an entry gate, so do not re-apply it to the objective already chosen" (m23 won, m12 lost, m20 block destroyed -> lead); "proximity is not combat while stalled and unhurt on a flag march" (8 worse, 2 better, three failures). A sixth attempt on the older open finding, the healed-back hulk — "a hulk that one volley finishes joins the focus pool" — was rejected too, and hardest of all: 58 scenarios worse, five failures. **Match 26 (05.09.2026, the first match started from the session with `tools/play.py`, lost on points 12721:23408 — player 1 from (12,9); an opponent that farms flags and never fights):** the series was run `-n 20 --stop-on-defeat` and stopped at the very first match. **Nobody died on either side in 1500 ticks — 902 hits of damage to us and 245 to them, out of 16000 each — and we were stronger the whole time (4075 against 3084, at one point 4179 against 2016).** Their army moved as one blob between the flags, ours followed it: `huntable=0/8` in the posture log — of eight combat enemies not one was catchable. Meanwhile the flags changed hands over and over: A3 at (31,67) eight times, D5 at (49,49) six, each cycle about forty ticks. We ended holding two flags (both under a scout that sits on them) to their four or five, 7-12 points a tick against 13-18. **The stall — the one mechanism that turns the army from a hunt back to the flags — never fired: it counts a picket of at most `STALL_PICKET` (four) armed enemies in reach, and a blob of six to twelve cancelled it by sheer number.** The stub gained a `farm` script for this opponent, and it reproduces the defeat on seven of ten maps: on map 21 three flags (two H4 and an A3, eleven points a tick) stood unowned for 1500 ticks while our army chased the blob, 4413:16323 without a single death. **The fix counts the picket among the enemies we can actually catch.** What we can catch is a fight; what evades us is not a reason to stand still — and the twenty-tick window with no net damage either way already tells a real army apart, since one that has been within `ENGAGE_RANGE` for twenty ticks has opened fire. Two rejected first: dropping the count entirely (m17 wing went from the enemy army destroyed at 435 to a points loss 10619:22564) and, on the keeper side, both "the picket only counts while a fight is on" (5 worse, m24 spread win -> loss) and "assign a keeper at the same radius that holds one" (43 worse, five failures — every extra keeper costs a fighter). On the farm suite the fix turns maps 24 and 20 from a points loss into a win and costs map 23; the main suite keeps every scenario and softens two (m20 block, m20 wing). match-3 — kite destroyed at 222, army destroyed at 450, rush destroyed at 238, sleeper destroyed at 683, none lead 5259:0 at 1790; match-2 — scouts lead 19055:14090 at 1802, grab destroyed at 206, rush destroyed at 181; match-1 — grab lead 24073:19451 at 1816, rush lead 23703:947 at 1090, scouts lead 19058:14095 at 1802; 5 failures, errors 0.

**Match 27 (05.09.2026, v28's first series — two wins, then the army lost at t=400 while 1086:0 ahead on points; player 1 from (12,9)):** the enemy took no flag at all the whole match — it scored zero and went straight for the army, which wins outright whatever the score. We held R3 from t=39, and **that is what made us the weaker side: a flag debuffs its owner, and R3's `R×0.8` put us at 3967 against their 4087.** They closed at t=100 (`approach=94`), contact at t=119 with retreat no longer feasible, and the fight at **0.97** was lost five creeps to three; the remnants were hunted down by t=280. The exchange itself is the defect, not the decision to fight: at t=133 their four melee were alive (M8A7, M8A6, M8A5, M8A8) while three of ours were dead or fully disarmed (M8A1, M7, M6) and all five of our ranged stood near full health — we traded our melee for their ranged.
