**Matches 174–179 (06.09.2026, the first series of v70): 5:1 — あぶらむし twice in 1 100 and 1 800 ticks, stachu3478 in 200, けろびー in 1 200 (1150 -> 1159), Xeon_Lagunas in 1 100, then けろびー's fighting build once more, the army gone by t=210 at his 11 240 of 12 000 (1159 -> 1152). Recorded; the operator stopped the loop here.** The top-edge fight of matches 140, 150, 163 and 173 again: evade east along the top edge, flee points (54,7) and (57,7), contact at t=75 at parity, the exchange even and then ours to t=150 (11 186 against 9 532, three of his dead), and eleven creeps became three between t=150 and 180. The replay: four or more of our shots on one target in 1 % of our firing ticks against his 11 % — v70's tier chose the reachable target and still one or two guns reached it, because our ranged stood at four or more with a free cell ahead in 141 creep-ticks of the first seventy-five and his ranged at three (32 % of ours within three of an enemy against 21 % of his, yet 75 of our ranged creep-ticks silent-disarmed against his 39); our target choice 100 % into an armed ranged in reach, his 66 %; our 231 shots to his 201. Every number of this fight is the one of the day's five losses to this build: the exchange is won on shots and lost on where they land. What is left open at the end of the session, in one line each: concentration is placement (the ranged a row too far, the free cell ahead never taken — the standing-line finding of matches 30, 53, 67, 145, 153, 160 and this one); the corner post and the flee that trembles at the edge; the push that does not close on a parked blob (70, 133, 152, 159); and the parity doctrine's exception for a lost race against a silent opponent (v63), which the operator has yet to confirm or revert.

**v71–v74 (06.09.2026, the stub work of the operator's plan — "5, then 1 and 2", no live series): the stub learns the standing line's fire concentration (`enemy conc:`), a focusing screen (`screen+focus`) and a silent camper that steps aside (`camp+shy`); one prey for the whole push (v71) is rejected, the quiet farmer's pack as no obstacle (v72) and the farmer's clock from NEAR (v72b) measure neutral and stay, the ranged row measured from his ranged (v73, v73b) is rejected by the gate twice, and a contact without an exchange is no fight for the stall nets and the detachments (v74) — kept.** Item 5, the stub model of the standing line: the runner now counts the enemy script's concentration the way `tools/replay.py` counts it live and prints it at the end; `screen+focus` puts every gun of the screen on our most forward creep with its melee at two as a poke shield, and still does not hold — destroyed at t=180–210 on maps 25, 28, 30–32 and 34 with its four-plus share 0–17 % (live 11–28 %) and ours 2.0–2.35 shots on one target per firing tick, while the plain `screen` never fires on maps 31 and 34 (its formation gate deadlocks); the stub's contact is a home-corner brawl, not the line at three or four of matches 140–179, and that line stays unmodelled. `camp+shy` — the parked blob steps aside from our armed within six, walks back and never fires — reproduces the camper losses of 133, 152 and 159 on v70: maps 34, 29 and 31 won (23306:7341, 23829:15654, 23614:20458), 30 and 33 lost (9615:23822, 22494:23779). Item 2 on that model, map 30: eight hundred ticks of pushing=true at parity, contact=true, twelve of twelve huntable, the army nine cells from his centroid and every creep step=stay — the melee with one engage target, the ranged with another, compactness holding both at the centre. v71, one prey for everyone in the push: gate 125/125 at 4 worse / 9 better, but camp+shy went from three wins of five to one (map 29 23829:15654 -> 16112:23821, map 31 23614:20458 -> 13698:23470 — the army followed one retreating creep and lost its flags) — off. v72, the pack on a flag is no obstacle for a farmer quiet FARMER_QUIET ticks since he first came NEAR (live map 31's core at parity walked to the corner flags forty cells away while he sat on D5): gate against v70 only map 33 farm+weak −9 679, camp+shy unchanged; v72b moves the farmer's clock from the first reach (eight cells) to the first NEAR — the shy camp keeps nine and never enters eight: gate identical, map 33 gets one detachment and is still lost — both stay on, unmeasured live. Item 1, concentration is placement, measured before building: over eleven cached replays, one of our ranged standing at four or more from his nearest ranged has in most creep-ticks NO free neighbour cell within three of him (map 179's exchange, t=75–155: 265 of 327; a step allowed by every filter of the plan 32, blocked by the our-side rule alone 23) — the single-creep step is a patch for a sixth of it, and the cause is the row: with the focus row off (v45) the ranged row stands at 3 − d from the NEAREST threat, which against the fighting build is the poke melee at two, so the row is one behind the front and four to six from his ranged. v73 takes d from his nearest ranged: gate 125/125 at 17 worse / 7 better (rush/nine/block on maps 13, 28, 31–33 from destruction to a lead, camp 34 817 -> 1 615, army 5 161 -> 223); v73b only when his ranged is within four of the front: 14 worse / 9 better — the row level with the front reaches his ranged at the price of his melee at two from our ranged, and on the stub the price is higher than the gain; both off (`USE_ROW_TO_RANGED`), the live test of the fighting build's line is the operator's call. v74, the deadlock of map 30 named: the picket net does not fire (more than STALL_PICKET of his catchable within eight — "his army, not a picket"), and the march-stall net, the keeps-distance window and the detachment loop were all switched off by the word contact — one of his creeps within four of one of ours, without a shot from either side for eight hundred ticks. Contact is a fight only when we fired or were hit within STALL_TICKS (`lastFireTick`, `USE_COLD_CONTACT`); "standing in contact is legitimate — the line brawls in place under full mutual healing" still holds, because a brawl fires. Gate 125/125 at 0 worse / 1 better (map 32 farm+weak +6 911); camp+shy: map 30 from 9615:23822 to a win 23824:21388, map 31 from 23614:18608 to a loss 13931:23616 (two detachments by t=460, a picket stall to 780, and from t=800 the core of eight at HOLD for eight hundred ticks at 7 against 18 while his twelve sat on D5 — the open "core at parity in front of a shy blob" of maps 29 and 31), 29, 33 and 34 unchanged. Five maps are too few for a chaotic model, so camp+shy was run on all thirty-three maps as player one, toggle on against off: wins 12 to 12, summed margin −68 250 -> −22 506, margins moved by a thousand or more 8 better / 5 worse, flips two each way (maps 13 and 28 to wins by +17 819 and +10 174, maps 6 and 31 to losses by −3 983 and −14 691) — kept on that, and the core that holds at parity in front of a shy blob is the next item.

**Matches 180–183 (06.09.2026, the first series of v74): 3:1 — けろびー twice in 1 200 and 100 ticks (1152 -> 1161), System in 1 700, then けろびー's farmer, lost on points 20 315:23 272 in 1 800 ticks (1161 -> 1152), the seventeenth loss to the farmer.** His army scattered over the flags from tick 1 (guards of six and ten on two flags at t=100); ours, 4 179 against 2 890, hunted and killed — 16 000 of his hits were 4 400 by t=700 — and the score was 4 300:11 787, the rate 3–12 against 13–25 for seven hundred ticks. From t=650 his armed were two lone runners (the replay: his largest armed group at most half of his armed in 89 % of the ticks, a flag cell visited every twenty to thirty ticks — D5, H4, R3, A3) at a cell a tick, and our nine armed ran after them in one group at 0–8 against 17–25, the posture flickering ANNIHILATE/HOLD/FLAG every ten to thirty ticks with huntable 3/3 and 0/3 by turns, not one shot of ours after t=637. The detachment came at t=935, the tick FARMER_QUIET had passed since the last hit on us (t=635, the fight that killed his creeps), and from then on the rate was 21:4, 22:3, 25:0; the gap at t=935 was 10 600 and the loss 2 957. v75, the dry hunt: we have fired, since then PASSIVE_TICKS without a shot of ours or a hit on us, behind on score, and his armed SCATTERED — the largest group within ENGAGE_RANGE at most half of them — enters the farmer chain without the three-hundred-tick quiet, and the core keeps PUSH_RATIO over his army instead of parity. What it is not, measured: "a hundred ticks without an exchange" alone released the detachment at t=100 against an intact parked camp (the clock ran from tick 1 and "never hit" gave the parity floor: maps 30, 31, 33 and 34 camp lost) and against the stub's kiter walking as five (map 31 kite 1 979:23 896 — twenty-five ticks of detachment at t=183–214 and the fight without four is lost), gate 120/125. With "we have fired" and "scattered": gate 125/125 identical to v74, camp+shy map 31 back to a win 23 620:17 977, spread map 33 from 19 509:24 315 to 24 327:24 222, the rest unchanged; the stub has no scenario of two runners re-taking flags behind a hunt, so the number this stands on is the live one. Seen on the way and left as it is: `spread` on maps 19, 28 and 31 is 3 207, 16 732 and 8 967 against 24 300 with fourteen alive on both sides, on both builds.

**Matches 184–185 (06.09.2026, the first series of v75): 1:1 — Hardy in 1 100 (1152 -> 1154), then MetalicaX, a points loss 20 986:22 020 at t=1900 of 1 958 ticks with both armies intact (1154 -> 1143).** A blob of nine armed that never fights: it walked between its A3 (67,31) and R3 (85,49) at seven to fifteen cells from us (the replay: his largest armed group all nine in every tick, one exchange of hits, at t=243), holding four flags to our three — 13 against 12 a tick for a thousand ticks. Ours, 1.2 by the push measure, was pushing, huntable 12/12 and 0/12 by turns as he stepped back, and the posture flickered ANNIHILATE/HOLD every five or six ticks from t=500 to 1200 without a shot. The chase window of v57 counts ANNIHILATE ticks only and is cleared by every HOLD, so in 1 900 ticks it never reached DETACH_WINDOW (dry=2503 at the one detachment, t=1503, which came by the lost-race entry after the three-hundred-tick quiet); the picket stall at t=1212 sent the army to the far corner, where it stood at (18,67) for 250 ticks in HOLD while his blob retook D5 — the corner post, open since v70. v76: the window survives a HOLD that is a paused chase — no catchable enemy and his armed within ENGAGE_RANGE + RANGED_RANGE. Two wider forms measured and rejected: any HOLD (gate 123/125 — camp on maps 31 and 33 from destruction at 375–384 to points losses, the detachments leaving the post before the push), and HOLD without catchable enemies (124/125 — map 31 camp 7 520:23 669, the detachment at t=113 at the post while his blob farmed forty-five cells away). With "near" as well: gate 125/125 identical to v75, camp+shy and spread unchanged — the stub has no blob that roams at parity beside us without fighting, so this stands on the live number; at parity the pool releases one creep (the core keeps PARITY_FLOOR over his 3 507), and whether one more runner turns 12:13 around is for the next match to say.

**Matches 186–187 (06.09.2026, the first series of v76): 2:1 — werwie in 300, けろびー in 1 000 (1143 both), then MetalicaX again, a points loss 17 152:23 682 at t=1700 of 1 730 ticks (1143 -> 1134), and not one line of any net in the whole match.** The same blob of nine, but this time it kept five to nine cells from us — under the ENGAGE_RANGE the keeps-distance window asks for — and gave a little: four of his creeps died in 1 700 ticks. We pushed at 1.1, the behind-on-score allowance, for the whole match, and every time the army left D5 for the blob his scout took the flag behind it: D5 changed hands at t=484, 641, 704, 1057 and 1418; in the last three hundred ticks the rate was 8:17 where the army on the flag had held 13:12 through t=800–1400. The opening cost the rest: his blob came at parity from tick 1, we evaded to the corner (96,96) by the rule of v29, and it walked over our R3 (85,49) at t=88 and H4 (90,8) at t=122 — 2 558 behind by t=500. v77: the push allowance below PUSH_RATIO (1.1 behind, 1.0 in a stalemate) exists for points and was giving them away — while the army holds a flag by presence (the own flag of v66 within POST_STANDOFF) and there is no exchange (fightOn), the ratio is PUSH_RATIO; a push that is a fight, or one at a clear advantage, goes as before. Gate 125/125 identical to v76, camp+shy and spread unchanged — the stub has no opponent that gives ground at five cells and farms with scouts behind our army, so this too stands on the live number. Left open, in one line each: the opening evade at parity from a blob that takes flags on its way (v29 was measured against one that took none), and the first MetalicaX match's question — whether one detached runner turns 12:13 around.

**Matches 188–190 (06.09.2026, the first series of v77): 2:1 — G1N6ERbreadMan in 1 000 (1134 -> 1136), けろびー in 200 (-> 1141), then MetalicaX a third time, 19 755:23 543 at t=1800 of 1 848 ticks with no contact at all (1141 -> 1132).** From t=300 nothing moved: our army on D5 (v77 kept it there), his blob of nine parked beside his A3 (67,31) seventeen cells away, the rate 12:13 for fifteen hundred ticks after an opening of −1 800 (the corner evade at parity again; his blob walked over our R3 at t=84 and H4 at t=121, six flags by t=121). And his R3 (13,49) sat unoccupied for 1 300 ticks — three points a tick, 3 900, the whole margin — while our three runners sat: scout_1 on A3 (31,67), scout_2 on H4 (8,90), and the one creep the parity pool released at t=512 on D5 under the army's nose. A runner's value was a rate over the approach, gain / (ticks + 5), so sitting on an own three-point flag (0.5 × 3 / 5 × 1.25 = 0.375) beat a six-point swing twenty-five ticks away (6 / 30 = 0.2) with 1 300 ticks to go. v78: in a quiet game (farmerQuietNow — he has not hit us for FARMER_QUIET ticks since he first came near: match 190 from t=445, the campers of 133, 152 and 159) the value is the gain over the remaining horizon, gain × (ticks left − ticks of approach); in the opening and in a fight the rate stays. Measured on the way: the horizon always — gate 123/125 at 27 worse / 33 better, points races far better (screen+flagless map 31 5 118:0 -> 21 314:336, farm+weak +13 243 and +8 576, grab +5 592) and a dozen destructions turned into leads (ahead on points, no sweep), but kite maps 13 and 28 lost the army: the runners take the corner flags earlier, the army is left D5 beside the kiter's A3, and the push at a pre-contact estimate of 1 644 against 428 walks into his formed line (700 against 811 in contact) — with the old value the army went for the corner H4, the kiter came to D5 and died in the open: the finding of v28, not of the horizon. Sitting worth half only with an enemy within KEEP_RANGE (v78b) — gate 125/125 but camp+shy 0 of 5 (map 34 23 306:7 341 -> 16 932:24 017): the scouts leave while his runners are far, and the runners take the flags. The quiet-game form: gate 125/125 at 1 worse (map 34 camp, a destruction into a lead) / 3 better (farm+weak +5 219 and +3 499, grab +4 678), and camp+shy over all thirty-three maps as player one, on against off: wins 13 -> 21, summed margin +234 -> +59 540, margins moved by a thousand or more 18 better / 11 worse, flips nine to wins and one to a loss (map 8, 23 312:14 452 -> 21 290:23 299).

**Match 191 (06.09.2026, the first match of v78): 0:1 — Coldkimchi, the fighting line's tenth win over this bot, the army gone by t=200 at his 15 994 of 16 000 (1132 -> 1127).** Parity 4 087:4 087; his blob came from the centre at our corner, and for ninety ticks the evade changed its target five times between the corner (96,96) (exit 0) and the far H4 (8,90) (arrive −42: he is there forty-two ticks before us) — from a corner with no exit the point is marked left (evadeLeft), the flee direction has nowhere to go (fleePoint null), and the least bad of the bad remains, a march through him; at t=71 the corner is best again and the army walks back. Contact in the corner at t=94, and the fight of matches 140–179 once more: his melee at two or three from ours in 72–78 % of their creep-ticks and 63 swings to our 9 (ours at four or more, a third of the time at seven), our ranged silent-disarmed 45 creep-ticks to his none, our shots into his melee 89 and into his ranged 75 (a hundred per cent into an armed ranged when one was in reach — there rarely was: our ranged at four or more from his nearest ranged 239 creep-ticks, 116 of them with no free cell within three of him, and of the rest the step refused by his melee within two in 46), four or more of ours on one target in 0 % of firing ticks against his 12 %. v79, a point he reaches first is no evade target — measured and rejected: gate 125/125 but 13 worse / 9 better, and screen+flagless on map 32, the model of the flagless Coldkimchi of matches 30–31 that the evade of v29 was built on, 21 339:1 215 -> 17 079:13 627 — without a point the army holds at the post and the line comes to it; camp+shy maps 29 and 31 from wins to losses (spread 19 and 30 to wins). It would not have changed match 191 either: from a corner at equal speed he wins the race to every point but the one behind our back, and the choice is a fight in the corner or a march into him — the fight's quality decides, and that is the ranged row a step too far (v73, off), which the stub cannot judge and a live trial can. Off (`USE_EVADE_NO_LOSING_RACE`); v79 plays as v78.

**Matches 192–193 (06.09.2026, the series of v79, which plays as v78; the series had to be started three times — twice the client's page answered game/start with "Failed to fetch" while its GETs worked): 1:1 — Xeon_Lagunas in 1 100 (1127), then MetalicaX a fourth time, 22 138:23 728 at t=1900 with nine of ours dead by then (1127 -> 1119).** The opening as before — the corner evade, his blob over five flags by t=88, −1 500 by t=200 — and then from t=900 to 1700 both armies stood at parity, 3 507 against 3 679, ours on four flags to his three: 15 against 10 a tick, from −5 300 at t=900 to −1 374 at t=1700, by projection +126 at the end; the scouts sat on R3 and H4 because every flag of his lay under his blob's reach (the pack) or behind the parity gate (0.953 < 0.97). At t=1700 the last call — LAST_CALL_TICKS with ourScore ≤ enemyScore and no projection — opened captures at 0.95: the army left for A3 (31,67), the ranged sitting on D5 was released as a detachment the same tick, his scout stepped onto D5, and his blob came to D5 and took nine of ours (13 946 -> 3 362 against his 16 000 -> 13 484). v80: the last call fires when the projection to the end is not ours — score plus the rate difference times the ticks left at or below zero; the 0:0 draw at equal rates stays covered. Gate 125/125 identical, camp+shy and spread unchanged: no stub line reaches the last three hundred ticks behind on score and ahead by projection, so this stands on the live number. The day's four MetalicaX matches in one line: the corner evade of v29 at parity has cost the opening every time (−1 500 to −2 558 by t=200–500), and each match was then a race the middle game won and the ends lost — the opening against a blob that takes flags on its way stays the open item.

**Matches 194–195 (06.09.2026, the first series of v80): 1:1 — けろびー in 300 (1119 -> 1124), then Coldkimchi, the fighting line's eleventh win, the army gone by t=480 at his 15 908 of 16 000 (1124 -> 1120).** This time from the north-west corner: the evade went to (3,3), then along the top edge to (96,3) and H4 (90,8) with arrive −31 to −35 — he would be there first, and did not come: his blob toured the map for 280 ticks without taking a flag (the flagless Coldkimchi of matches 30–31), our army took H4 (90,8) at t=123 and sat on it at 4:0, and our scouts took nothing — the flagless-rush signal of v29 closes captures while his blob is at sixty to seventy-seven cells (six free flags for 280 ticks, an open item). The fight at H4 from t=274 to 480, the same as ten times before: his melee at two or three from ours (2:242, 3:340 creep-ticks) and 35 swings to our 6 — our four melee adjacent to an enemy in 9 of 701 creep-ticks; our ranged 370 shots to his 482, silent-disarmed 77 creep-ticks to his none, four or more on one target 0 % against 8 %. The step measurement refined over three replays (179, 191, 195): of our ranged creep-ticks at four or more from his nearest ranged, 272/104/285 stand at five or more — a row or two behind — 44/52/144 have a free step allowed by every filter of the plan and do not take it, and only 10/12/32 are blocked by our own creeps. The rows of planBlock are the planner in force while his line steps back (enemyRetreating), and they put the ranged row at 3 − d from the nearest threat — his poke melee at two. v81: the row measured from his nearest ranged when it is within four of the front (`USE_ROW_TO_RANGED`, the v73b form) goes live — the gate is green (125/125 at 13 worse / 10 better: destructions into leads and slower kills, no loss), camp+shy and spread unchanged, and the stub cannot hold this line; the live series is the measurement, per the standing "implement the findings and try".

**Matches 196–197 (06.09.2026, the first series of v81): 1:1 — Lumbo in 1 800 (1120), then けろびー's farmer, the eighteenth loss to him, 17 848:23 225 in 1 771 ticks (1120 -> 1112).** His army scattered over six flags by t=83; ours hunted at 1.3 rising to 2.8 and killed eight by t=700 — at 6–12 against 13–19 a tick, −8 600 by then. From t=450 what was left of him was one blob of five (the replay: his largest armed group all five in every tick, 0.36 of our power, a cell a tick) that toured the flags and never fired again after t=837, and our twelve ran after it for four hundred ticks without a shot; the dry hunt of v75 asks for a SCATTERED remnant, so the detachment came at t=1103 by the three-hundred-tick quiet instead of about t=937 — and from t=1103 the rate was 16:9, 19:6, 21:4, from −12 465 to −8 358 by t=1500, the detachments recalled at t=1568 by one hit, 25:0 at t=1600. v82: the dry hunt without "scattered", and the kiter's guard moved into the pool — a detachment held by the dry hunt alone releases a creep only while the core keeps PUSH_RATIO over his army AND its ranged mass keeps PUSH_RATIO over his ranged (melee do not catch a kiter), and only after PASSIVE_TICKS of hunting without FLAG, EVADE or RETREAT. Measured on the way: the ranged guard alone (v82) — 124/125, map 29 camp 9 716:23 817: the pool used the parity floor because "never hit" is quiet too, and five left at t=226 against his full blob after one brush at t=114; the floor by entry (v82b) — still 124/125 (11 016:23 817): the army had been in FLAG from t=168 to 208 and hunting for nineteen ticks; hunting for PASSIVE_TICKS required of every entry (v82c) — gate 125/125, but spread map 33 24 327:24 222 -> 19 509:24 315, the scattered entry delayed. Required of the guarded entry only (v82d): gate 125/125 identical to v81, camp+shy and spread unchanged. The scattered opening (six flags by t=83 against our hunt) stays what it was: −8 600 while killing eight, the doctrine's price, and the open question of whether a hunt that kills should also farm.

**Match 198 (06.09.2026, the first match of v82): 0:1 — Coldkimchi, the fighting line's twelfth win, the army gone by t=480 at his 15 802 of 16 000 (1112 -> 1108), and the one live match of the ranged-row trial of v81.** The shape of 195 to the tick: the corner evade to (3,3), then along the top edge to (96,3) and H4 (90,8) with arrive −31 to −42, his flagless blob touring for 280 ticks without a flag, our army on H4 at 4:0 from t=119, and the fight from t=283 to 480 at (79,13)–(66,28). Our ranged 427 shots to his 468 — the same number of guns in reach a tick, about two each — but four or more on one target in 0 % of our firing ticks (in 227 of 271 at most one shot on any target) against his 4 %; his shots into our ranged 182 and our healers 100, ours into his melee 180; our melee swung 5 times to his 33, adjacent to an enemy in 13 of 602 creep-ticks; our ranged silent-disarmed 79 creep-ticks to his none. And the row's number: our ranged at TWO cells from his nearest creep 208 creep-ticks against 119 in match 195 without the row, at four or more still 404 — the row level with the front brought the ranged to his poke melee at two, not to his ranged at three behind it. One match is one sample; the row is off again (v83 plays as v82 with `USE_ROW_TO_RANGED` off), and the finding for this line sharpens: the fight is not lost on shots (427 to 468) but on where they land — his into our ranged and healers, ours into his healed melee — and on four melee of ours that never swing.

**Match 199 (06.09.2026, the first match of v83): 0:1 — MetalicaX a fifth time, 10 996:17 460 at t=1700 of 1 922 ticks (1108 -> 1098).** From t=400 to 700 the army held near (16,67) at 10:8 with both armies whole (3 559 against 3 630); at t=763 his healer stepped onto our A3 (31,67), the race turned lost for a tick, and the quiet chain released three ranged — the pool checked the core against his power measured against the FULL army (3 222), while his power against the core of nine is 3 520 (Lanchester pairs: fewer ranged of ours, more of his power counts), so the core stood at 0.84 with 0.97 "verified". A core weaker than him is hunted: EVADE for nine hundred ticks in the corner (15,76), 228 evade decisions, while his blob did not move a cell in 900 ticks at forty-two cells; the three detached ranged stood beside it in RESERVE the whole time — no flag a runner would go for (his healers on D5 and A3, his blob on R3) — and the rate was 6:12 to the end. v84: the pool measures his power against the core candidate, not the full army; a detachment whose runners have all been without a target for DETACH_WINDOW ticks is recalled, and a new one waits the same window. Gate 125/125 identical, spread map 33 24 327:24 222 -> 24 328:21 821, camp+shy map 33 19 070 -> 18 291 with the recall cycling every hundred ticks (sixteen detach lines: nothing for a runner to take under a parked blob), the rest unchanged. Left open: the core at 0.84 hiding for nine hundred ticks from a blob that never moved — the flag objective under his twelve (v72) and the evade that trembles both live in that corner.

**Matches 200–201 (06.09.2026, the first series of v84): 1:1 — けろびー in 100 (1098 -> 1104), then MetalicaX a sixth time, about 20 800:22 100 in 1 922 ticks (1104 -> 1097).** The opening once more: the corner evade at t=3 and 33 — before his first flag at t=40, which is when the flagless-rush signal drops — and by t=122 his blob and scouts held all seven flags while our army walked from the corner to H4 (8,90) eighty cells away; −2 000 by t=200, at 1.36 by the power measure (his five debuffs), a hunt that alternated with the far flag and closed on nothing. The middle: HOLD on D5 at 12:13, then 15:10 from t=600 with four flags; at t=800 his blob took D5 behind the army that had moved to (29,61), and the dry hunt released five, recalled at t=894 (nothing to take), two again at t=944, five at t=1184 — the runners took A3, R3, H4 and A3 by t=1317 and the debuffs of four flags brought the core of seven from 2 824 to 2 347 against his 3 630: hunted, EVADE in the corner (3,28) from t=1300 to 1600 while his blob sat at (28,68), and three of the five detached ranged stood beside the core in RESERVE for two hundred ticks — two of the five held flags, so "all without a target" never came. The rate stayed ours (13:12 to 15:10) and the opening's 2 000 were never recovered. v85: a detached runner without a target for DETACH_WINDOW ticks returns to the core by itself. Gate 125/125 identical; camp+shy map 29 16 986:23 822 -> 23 824:23 434 and map 30 18 967 -> 23 609:23 828, spread map 19 3 207 -> 18 779, the rest unchanged. The day's ledger: 1152 -> 1097 over twenty-eight matches, nine losses to two opponents — MetalicaX six times on points, the corner opening each time, and Coldkimchi's line three times by annihilation — and the two structural items are named: the opening at parity against a blob that takes flags on its way, and the fight against the line (shots land on the wrong side, four melee never swing).

**Matches 202–206 (06.09.2026, the first series of v85): 4:1 — MetalicaX in 1 900 (1097 -> 1105; the first win over him in seven, 21 307:21 276 with 15:10 in the last hundred ticks after a picket stall at t=521 sent the army to the flags and 18:7 from t=600, the dry hunt's detachments from t=875 with one runner recalled by itself at t=1004), けろびー in 300 (-> 1111), houshanyun in 1 700, あぶらむし in 1 700 (1111), then G1N6ERbreadMan's melee rush, the army gone by t=280 (1111 -> 1099).** The fourth cornered fight of the day: his flagless blob came straight at parity, the evade of v29 went to (85,88), then (96,96) at exit −4, (90,8) at arrive −46, back and forth, (49,49) at arrive −22 — ten evade decisions, the last four toward him — and contact at t=124 in our corner (93,92). The brawl itself was even or ours: our melee swung 80 times to his 44, his ranged silent-disarmed 52 creep-ticks to our 4, four or more of ours on one target in 13 % of firing ticks to his 0 %; but 50 of our 199 attacks went into his healers (27 melee swings and 23 shots) against 14 of his 68 into ours, our armed died first, and at t=240 five of ours with 6 000 hits and no weapon faced his six with 7 080. No code change: the evade rule that would have refused the points he reaches first is v79, rejected by the stub's flagless screen; the healer as a target is HEALER_VALUE (v31), measured then against けろびー. The day's cornered fights — 191, 195, 198, 206 — are one finding: at parity against a rush that takes no flags, the evade at equal speed ends in our corner, and the corner is the worst ground we have; whether to stand in the open instead is the doctrine's question, the operator's to answer.

**v94 (06.09.2026, the operator's next two items): one measure of the core, and detachments in pairs.** The pool checked the core against his power only in the tick of release; a hundred ticks later the flags had changed hands (the holder's debuffs), the distances and reach with them, and the posture saw 0.82 where the pool had checked 0.97 — the core of nine hiding in EVADE in matches 199, 201 and 233. Now the core holds the floor EVERY tick by the posture's own measure: when it falls under, the strongest detached creep is recalled until it holds, and the next release waits DETACH_WINDOW as after a recall for want of a target — without that pause the recall and the release swung every tick (camp+shy map 33 fifty-six detachment lines). Pairs: a flag whose guard one runner cannot beat but the two nearest free armed runners can (Lanchester of the pair against the pack, plus the occupant) gets both, and the pool releases what the targets' guards demand — two for a flag with an armed creep of his within ENGAGE_RANGE, one otherwise. Measured apart and together: the measure alone — gate 125/125 at 6 worse / 4 better, camp+shy four of five, spread five of six; the pairs alone — 1 worse / 1 better, camp+shy four of five, spread four of six (map 31 to a loss: pairs without the measure over-release); both — 5 worse / 5 better (roost on maps 28 and 29 to leads, camp 31 slower, camps 29 and 34 faster), camp+shy four of five (map 30 -> 23 372:11 255), spread six of six (map 34 18 415:24 326 -> 24 332:22 234). **Match 238 (06.09.2026, the first match of v95): 0:1 — けろびー as a blob, annihilated by t=125 at his 15 492/16 000, 0:165 (1086 -> 1081).** Mirror armies (4087:4087) and his whole army marching at us from the first tick — not the farmer of matches 119–233. We evaded to the corner, walked to the post at R3 (13,49) and met him in the open at (11,33) on t=67 at exact parity. The replay (t=66–125): his 249 shots and 77 swings against our 120 and 11; his melee adjacent to an enemy 31 % of creep-ticks, ours 17 %; with three healers a side (216 heal per tick on a target) our ~130 damage per tick never broke his healing and his ~600 broke ours every four ticks. Two mechanisms, both ours. First, his ranged walked to exactly three from our front melee and stood (t=68–74, his melee at 4–7): the melee held the line (holdMelee engages only at two), the press was withheld by the 'his melee approaching' rules (theirMeleeMid, theirMeleeClosing — m34/m56), and melee_4 lost 660 hits in five ticks for nothing; the designed trade of v30/m56 and left as is. Second, from t≈80 our line walked backwards for forty-five ticks — the armed centroid (13,32) -> (8,18) with his blob following at 3–4: the plan (planFight, 'standing fight' by v47/v64) picked the ranged cells with a target at three and no enemy melee within two, those cells ran from his advancing melee every tick, the screen and the healers followed the ranged, and his melee at two never became adjacent (equal speed) so the brawl exception never switched the plan off. On top of it the press give-up ('keeps its distance': the distance from our melee did not shrink over three ticks) marked all four of his melee on t=79–80 and every three ticks after (74 press lines in the fight) — because OUR melee were stepping back — and a marked enemy is refused by the engage and the poker alike (`it.id !in pressGiveUp`): our melee could not attack the melee that were killing us. The fix is v96.

**v96 (06.09.2026, by the finding of match 238): a standing fight is one where we stand, and a target that keeps its distance is one that leaves.** Two rules, toggles USE_STANDING_LINE_HOLDS and USE_GIVEUP_HE_LEAVES. The plan (planFight) is used only while our armed centroid has not moved away from his current centroid by PRESS_CLOSING (2) cells over the patience window (3 ticks); a yielding line gets the rows behind the front melee (planBlock), whose front holds its cell and chops what comes within two. The press give-up requires the target itself to have moved away from the cell our nearest melee stood on at the start of the window — the stub's screen (m35) steps back and leaves; a blob following our retreat comes closer to that cell and is never given up. Measured apart: the line rule alone — gate 125/125 at 0 worse / 4 better (camp 29 lead -> destroyed@577, camp 30 and 31 faster, farm+weak 32 +11 200), camp+shy map 30 from a loss to 23 363:16 989 and map 33 from a win to 18 172:23 451 (four of five as before); the give-up rule alone — 125/125 at 5 worse / 7 better (rush 33 and wing 33, block 34 from destroyed to lead; kite 20 and 4, rush 35, wing 34, farm+weak 33 the other way — a fight rule shuffles the fight lines, as always), camp+shy unchanged. Both together: 125/125 at 5 worse / 9 better, camp+shy four of five, spread six of six unchanged. The stub has no enemy that walks after a retreating line, so the give-up rule is confirmed only by the replay; the live number is the next blob.

**v97 (06.09.2026, by the finding of match 240): against a scatter the recall measures the core against his largest group.** USE_SCATTER_RECALL_REF: when his largest armed group is at most half of his armed (`scattered`, v91), the one-core-measure recall (v94) uses his largest group at PUSH_RATIO as the reference — what can reach the core — while the release stays measured against his whole army at the parity floor: a strict release and a lenient recall, a hysteresis instead of the three-out-two-back cycle. Measured: gate 125/125 at 4 worse / 3 better (all roost lines: 28 and 29 from a lead to destroyed, 31/33/34/35 slower), camp+shy five of five for the first time (map 33 from 18 172:23 451 to 23 593:15 027, map 34 23 500:17 970), spread five of six (map 19 14 079:24 321 — the one opening runner recalled at t=42 instead of 26 and the match diverges from there, no later fight; map 30 24 338:11 681). The opening against a scatter stays an open finding.

**Match 241 (06.09.2026, the first match of v97): 0:1 — MetalicaX a fourteenth time of fifteen, about 21 500:23 700 in 1 899 at our 16 000 and his 14 400 (1076 -> 1072).** The known shape with one new number. His blob of nine toured D5 (41), R3 (44), A3 (67,31) (60) and H4 (90,8) (84) while we evaded to the corner until t=24 and took R3 (13,49) and H4 (8,90) with the scouts (51, 149). From t=88 the push: 1.26–1.58 for him, ANNIHILATE pushing, and from t=300 to 830 the army stood 8–13 cells from his blob parked at A3 (67,31) with reach 0/5 — one fight at t=440–460 (his melee killed, ledger +995), nothing else. The blob stepped back two or three cells whenever we came; every step made all of his creeps 'evasive' for the eight-tick chase window, huntable flickered 0/11 <-> 6/11, and the push went off and on 21 times in 530 ticks (HOLD -> the post at (10,69) in the west -> ANNIHILATE -> back), the formation holding six of nine for the stragglers each time. The unguarded D5 and A3 (31,67) were his for those 500 ticks at 7 against 18 a tick. The keeps-distance window (v92) never fired: it needs HIS centre to move a quarter of the window (12 cells in 50), and a shy blob moves 3–10. The picket fired only at t=829 when the blob went south, and in 80 ticks the army took R3 (85,49), A3, D5 and H4 (90,8) — 18 against 7 a tick — and closed the gap from -6 550 to -1 114 by t=1500; then the push came back (1571, 1593), the blob retook A3, R3 (85,49), D5 and even R3 (13,49) between 1517 and 1595 while we chased, and the last 300 ticks went 7:18 again.

**v98 (06.09.2026, by the finding of match 241): he keeps his distance however he does it.** USE_KEEPS_DISTANCE_ANY_MOVE: for the stall net the keeps-distance window (DETACH_WINDOW, 50 ticks of push without a fight, the distance not shrunk and beyond ENGAGE_RANGE) no longer requires his centre to have moved a quarter of the window; the detachment's distanceKept (quietChain) keeps that requirement (v57/v59). A standing camp we park at within three to five cells (camp 31) is not beyond ENGAGE_RANGE and stays out of the window; a march shrinks the distance. Measured: gate 125/125 at 0 worse / 3 better (block 28 from a lead to destroyed@660, camp 31 1 248 -> 775, army 32 456 -> 408), camp+shy five of five and spread five of six unchanged.

**Match 242 (06.09.2026, the first match of v98): 0:1 — MetalicaX a fifteenth time of sixteen, 4 611:12 360 in 1 600 with both armies whole (1072 -> 1066).** A new shape from him: only D5 (41) and A3 (31,67) (81), and then all twelve armed parked between them at (41,57) for 1 500 ticks — a sentinel, not a tourist. We took R3 (13,49) with the scout at 64 and stood on it in HOLD from t=88 to the end at 3967:3520 (1.13): the push needed 1.3 because we hold a flag (v77), the four free flags (both H4, A3 (67,31), R3 (85,49)) were all nearer to him than to us, so the pack at each was his whole army and the objective gate wanted the push ratio with a fight cost; no contact, no fight, no push — every stall net silent, 3 against 8 a tick for 1 500 ticks. The capture floor in a lost race is 0.75 (v63/v88) while the pack gate of the same objective stayed at 1.3 — the two gates of one march disagreed.

**v99 (06.09.2026, by the finding of match 242): our own flag does not raise the push ratio in a lost race.** USE_PUSH_KEEPS_FLAG_UNLESS_LOST: when losing by projection with no hit on us for PASSIVE_TICKS (lostRaceNow, the same test the capture floor uses), holding a flag no longer lifts the push ratio to 1.3 (v77) — a flag worth three a tick is worth less than the match, and at 1.13 the push goes. Stub-neutral: gate 125/125 at 0 worse / 0 better, camp+shy and spread identical to v98 to the point — no stub scenario combines an own flag, a lost race and an impossible push; the live number is the next MetalicaX. Rejected first (v99a, toggle USE_LOST_RACE_PACK_PARITY off): the pack at a free flag judged at the parity floor without a fight cost in a lost race — gate 124/125 (camp 29 from destroyed@584 to a loss 15 006:21 075) at 10 worse / 10 better, camp+shy four of five (map 34 14 148:23 010), spread five of six: at a standing camp the army walked to free flags instead of the push that destroyed it.

**Match 243 (06.09.2026, the first match of v99): 0:1 — MetalicaX a sixteenth time of seventeen, 21 684:23 898 in 1 900 with both armies whole (1066 -> 1062).** The closest of the day and a third shape from him. The opening again: EVADE on t=3 to the corner (85,88) until t=33; he took D5 (39), R3 (13,49) (43), A3 (31,67) (59) and H4 (8,90) (83) — four by t=83; we R3 (85,49) (63), D5 by a runner (71), A3 (67,31) (91), H4 (90,8) (162). D5 decided it: the runner left it, the army stood at the post 14–27 cells away at 1.08, and his whole blob walked onto D5 at t=118 and kept it until 1312 — 1 194 ticks of the five-a-tick flag. From t=427 (our A3 (31,67)) we held four flags against his three at 13 against 12 a tick, gaining one a tick on a gap of 1 900 — and at 3234:3885 (four debuffs on us, three on him; RETREAT_RATIO 1.15) the army was 'hunted' and spent t=466–1150 in EVADE from a blob parked at (22,64) fifty-five cells away, walking between its own A3 (67,31) and R3 (85,49) every twenty to thirty ticks (evade scores 1 and 19, approach 0). The last 600 ticks were a flag war around D5 and the two A3 (flips at 1176, 1257, 1312, 1362, 1381, 1404, 1446, 1473, 1514, 1548, 1653, 1877) that he won by 2 214. v99's rule never applied — the push ratio was never the blocker here. What decided the day's three MetalicaX matches is the same pair of open findings: the thirty-tick corner opening that hands him the first flags, and D5 — whoever sits on the centre flag with the army collects five a tick behind a parity that forbids the other side to come.

**v100 (06.09.2026, the operator's decision: the scatter on the stand and the opening without the corner).** Two things. The stand gets `scatter`, the match-240 scatterer built from the replay (see the README): on the six maps of the spread family it reproduces the live opening to the tick (his D5 at 38, R3 at 43, both A3 at 53/56, both H4 at 82; ours R3 at 42–49) and v99 wins all six only just — map 31 by 217, map 34 by 1 456, map 30 by 1 469, with the live shape inside (D5 ours at ~130, parity at 3339:3098, RETREAT at 400 at 3044:3554, −6 800 at t=700, the lead only at t=1992); the six lines join the gate. On the way a stand defect: the farmer/camp blob headed for the nearest flag that was not its own even with our creep standing on it, and with our army posted next to its nearest flag it danced there all match instead of farming the rest and parking — the post is now the nearest takeable flag (alone: gate 125/125 at 4 worse / 4 better, all camp and farm lines). The bot: USE_OPENING_AT_POST — the unflagged-rush signal stays whole (the first flag is theirs, hunted, the exit at objectives), but the EVADE it used to force is gone: the army stands at the post (the flag of our half, v88) with the scout on it and leaves only by the ordinary evade (an armed enemy within EVADE_RANGE). The first form, a signal range of fifty cells, was tried and dropped: a scatter's fan enters fifty cells by t=17 while 'massed' still holds, and the army evaded from t=17 to 36 into the same corner (scatter map 34). Measured apart from the stand fix: gate 125/125 at 29 worse / 51 better — the openings of every scenario change; in the worse column nine 'destroyed -> lead' lines (twelve 12, camp 29/30, army 31, block 32, kite 34, wing 34, fourteen 35, rush 5) and the screen+flagless margins (maps 25 and 30 11 800 -> 1 832), in the better column fifty-one lines of enemy armies destroyed sooner (army 3/5/21/23/24/29/30/34/35, nine 28/29/32/33, rush 28/29/33/34, kite, roost, hunter). camp+shy five of five, spread five of six (map 33 lost, map 19 won), scatter six of six by 3 700–8 300 (map 31 24 324:17 751 against 24 318:24 101, map 34 24 320:19 683 against 24 314:22 858). The live number is the next opening. (The code, the stand form and the README landed as 9a786ea; the gate lines and this paragraph followed — the landing script's docs step failed on an anchor and the commit went out without them.)

**Matches 244–249 (06.09.2026, the first series of v100): 5:1, the best series of the day (1062 -> 1088 -> 1079).** けろびー in 1 000, G1N6ERbreadMan in 1 100, けろびー in 100, System in 1 500, then ricardo18informatica2020 — the scatterer of match 240 the stand scenario was built from — in 1 700 (1080 -> 1088); and then けろびー as a blob, the form of match 238, annihilated in 300 (-> 1079). The opening at the post worked as designed in all six: no corner, the scout on our R3 from the first flag, the army where the game is. Match 249 was the blob brawl again, at the centre instead of the open: HOLD to the post from t=3, the ordinary evade at 27 (an armed of his within 25), HOLD at 32 when the approach rate fell to zero, contact at 43 at 4087:4087, ANNIHILATE by contact. We killed three of his melee by t=100 and the model had us ahead (1832:1350) — and we were wiped by t=160 at his 11 200/16 000. The replay (t=43–160): his 412 shots and 51 swings against our 277 and 15; his melee adjacent to an enemy 20 % of creep-ticks, ours 5 %; his healers adjacent 235 times, ours 178; our ranged disarmed 113 creep-ticks, his 42, with the same focus (2.08 targets a tick, share 0.66–0.68). The trace of the first ten ticks: our melee_4 stepped two cells ahead alone at t=44 and was focused from 1 600 to nothing by t=52 while the other three stood at three or four under his ranged (holdMelee); the press fired at 49, two went in, swung three or four times each and were focused; his fourth melee walked round the flank and swung at our ranged and healers eight ticks running (a->ranged 21, a->healer 15 over the fight; ours a->melee 11, healed by three adjacent healers). The give-up rule of v96 marked all twelve of his creeps at t=46–49 — his blob did step back a cell at first contact — and our melee could not take them for twenty ticks. This is the fight-quality item (Coldkimchi, matches 238 and 249): the blob brawl at parity is lost on mechanics the day's rules do not touch — melee that find soft targets, healers that stay adjacent, ranged that live armed — and the stand's rush/nine/block enemies are beaten by the same mechanics, so it cannot measure a fix. Open, and now the largest item.

**v101 (06.09.2026, the operator's list in order — 1: the blob brawl on the stand; 2: fight quality, first item).** The stand gets `brawl` (see the harness section), built from the replays of 238 and 249 in four iterations: a blob that dives through our line is shredded by mass fire in thirty ticks (twelve deaths t=94–124 on map 31); one that keeps its edge — formed advance, melee at the front, ranged three from our nearest armed and three from every other, healers behind the ranged, a three-tick step back at first contact — fights for 280–540 ticks and still dies on all eight maps. Before tuning further the engine was checked against the replays: the damage and healing the intents should have produced (10 per live RANGED part a shot, 30 per ATTACK part a swing, 12 per HEAL part adjacent) against the hits actually lost and regained — match 249: his 34 260 expected, 23 230 observed lost; ours 19 500 against 13 973; match 238 the same within overkill and overlapping heals. The stand's engine is faithful; the loss is in the intents, and it decomposes: his 361 shots and 51 swings against our 262 and 15, his 235 adjacent heals against our 178. Uptime by role (the new `ours act:` line on the stand, the same count from the replay live): our healers heal in 94 % of the ticks they stand next to a wounded mate (his 100 %), our ranged fire in 70 % of the ticks with a target in three (his 89 %; the rest disarmed), our melee swing in 83 % of their adjacent ticks (his 86 %) — but are adjacent only 18 creep-ticks against his 59 (5 % against 20 %). Melee adjacency is the gap, and the stand with the feint reproduces it (5–16 %). First item of the fight list, the lone melee two ahead of the mass (v31; melee_4 of match 249 dead in eight ticks): three forms measured. A gate alone — a melee takes a target at two only with a mate within three of it (v101a): gate 130/131 (camp 30 23 283:23 793) at 12 worse / 9 better, brawl adjacency unchanged. The gate with a join (v101b): 130/131 at 16 worse / 15 better — in both, camp 34, rush 34 and nine 31 go from destroyed to a points lead: the stand's blobs are beaten by single melee entries and holding them back gives the blobs away. The join alone, USE_MELEE_PAIR_ENGAGE: a melee whose mate already stands at two from a target reaches it from three, so two arrive together — gate 131/131 (the six scatter lines included) at 5 worse / 8 better (camp 29 margin 21 066:11 059 -> 23 808:23 369, wing 33 to a lead; nine 19, wing 29/30/35, fourteen 35, block 32 to destroyed), brawl adjacency up to 20 % on map 31, camp+shy five of five and spread five of six unchanged. The gate half stays in the code as USE_MELEE_PAIR_GATE = false.

**v102 (06.09.2026, the operator's list in order — 3: the centre flag; 5: the price of ranged fire; 7: the closed findings).** Item 3, USE_POST_ON_CENTRE: while D5 is ours the army's post is D5 itself, not the centroid of our flags — in match 243 the runner took D5 at 71, the army stood at the centroid (64,40) fourteen cells away, and his blob of twelve walked onto D5 at 118 and sat there to 1312 at a parity we never contested; in 242 he took it himself and parked beside it. Measured in the plan worktree on top of v101: gate 131/131 at 8 worse / 4 better (camp 29 and 30 from a lead to destroyed, camp 33 and 34 the other way, farm+weak 28 to a lead, scatter 30/33 margins down and 31 up), camp+shy five of five, spread five of six, scatter six of six, brawl unchanged. A doctrinal trial; the live number is the next sentinel. Item 5, USE_MELEE_ADJACENCY_SHARE, measured and rejected: the power model counts a melee's 240 a swing in full (four M8A8 are three quarters of a side's damage in it) where the replays of 238/249 have melee adjacent 20 % of creep-ticks for him and 5–8 % for us — 36 % of his damage and 19 % of ours — so R3 (−20 % ranged) costs the model 1 % of power and A3 7.5 %, the reverse of the fight. A share of 0.25 on the melee's damage (hits in full) passes the gate 131/131 but reshuffles everything (27 worse / 40 better) and collapses the races: spread six of six -> two of six (map 31 11 058:24 336), camp+shy five of five -> three of five, brawl map 32 our army destroyed. The flag prices flip (R3 from cheap to dear) under parity floors tuned for the old prices, and the captures the stand's races stand on are refused. The price is right by the replays and cannot be changed apart from the floors — an open finding with a number: 0.25 needs PARITY_FLOOR/CAPTURE_FLOOR retuned across every family. Item 4 (the shy blob) has no new code: v98's stall is the live test. Item 7: closed for good — the sleeper chase at 0.65 power (v12, before the parity doctrine), spread on maps 18/19/22/23 (five of six today, the lost map alternating), the corner opening (v100), the fruitless chase (the stall nets, v36–v98), the scatter core (v97), the keeps-distance window (v98).

**Matches 259–267 (06.09.2026, the first series of v105): 8:1, the best series on record (1078 -> 1135 -> 1129, rank 1).** けろびー's blob destroyed in 200 — the first win in that form after three annihilations (238, 249, 251) — then MetalicaX in 1 700 (the third win over him ever, 1088 -> 1099), けろびー's farmer in 1 500, あぶらむし forfeit, ricardo in 1 800, けろびー's blob destroyed again in 300 (-> 1116), ricardo in 1 700, Coldkimchi in 1 800 (the first win after 0:13, -> 1135), and then けろびー's blob a third time, annihilated in 200 (-> 1129). The three blob fights by the replay: in the two wins our uptime was 95–100 % by every role (healers next to a wounded mate, melee adjacent, ranged with a target) against his 37–55 % melee adjacency, expected damage 34 140:22 440 and 23 940:16 740 for us; in the loss 10 920:23 580 against us — shots 156:256 (our ranged with a target in three 68 % against his 99 %), swings 6:32, healers 79 %, 54 give-ups. Contact came at t=41 head-on at the centre with his blob formed and ours marching; the fight is decided by who takes the first volleys in formation, and this time it was him. The blob brawl is a coin now, not a certain loss; the opening geometry of the contact is the next thing to read in it.

**v106 (06.09.2026, by match 258): the keeps-distance window survives the push's flicker.** USE_CHASE_WINDOW_WIDE: the paused chase (a HOLD with nobody catchable) keeps the distance window alive while an armed of his is within twice ENGAGE_RANGE of ours, not ENGAGE_RANGE + RANGED_RANGE — in 258 the push followed his touring blob at nine to fourteen cells for a thousand ticks, the posture flickered to HOLD every ten to thirty ticks on the evasive window, and the fifty ticks of v98's stall never accumulated: not one stall in 1 700 ticks. Stand-neutral to the line (gate 131/131 at 0/0 against v105): the stand's blobs do not tour at twelve. The live number is the next tour.

**Matches 268–273 (06.09.2026, the first series of v106): 5:1 (1129 -> 1157 -> 1153, rank 1).** けろびー three times in 1 600, 1 100 and 1 200, G1N6ERbreadMan in 1 000, けろびー forfeit, then Coldkimchi's standing line, annihilated in 800 at his 14 400/16 000. Not a passivity loss: by the replay our uptime was 97–98 % (ranged with a target, healers next to a wounded mate) against his 89–98 %, shots 1 044 against 1 135, healing 80 880 against 86 496 expected — the gap is 91 shots and 34 melee swings (his melee swing in 98 % of their adjacent ticks; adjacency 1–2 % for both sides at his line), a grind of 28 net hits a tick over five hundred ticks. Where it was fought decided it: the unflagged rush at parity sent the army into the ordinary evade at t=49, the points ran (10,71) -> (5,94) -> (3,96) with HOLD/EVADE flickering on the approach rate (79–96), and the line met us at t=98 at the west edge — the army fought at x=3–8 with its back to the wall for the whole match.
